- **Affected files:** `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/navigation/LoginNavigation.kt`, `LoginNavigationViewModel.kt`, common navigation ViewModel tests, QA results and event/diagnostic coverage map.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Resolve event/breadcrumb source as the explicit source supplied by the current back stack, falling back to the previous visible destination when popping to a root destination whose current stack has no source. Added a common regression asserting cold Splash/app-launch attribution and Login/back-navigation event plus breadcrumb attribution after the Login→Welcome pop. Commit `9958d084bf40bcf24024058c4d084fa04c3e1749`.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung 2026-09-27. On the hash-matched build, cold Welcome reported exactly once with `splash`/`app_launch`; after Get Started and system Back, exactly one Welcome event and visible breadcrumb reported `login`/`back_navigation`. Rotation between those route probes emitted no extra screen view. `:feature:login:ui:iosSimulatorArm64Test` passed (8 tests, zero failures); `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed. Local APK and Samsung-pulled package hashes both `5c8fa064366ac9f03a642135e1e198babe8c9ad1e10415ff181a7296c2093adf`. Evidence: [post-fix case-9 retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-009-welcome-rotation-attribution-retest.txt); original failure and pre-fix candidate evidence remain linked above. This only verifies the local debug Analytics provider; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived/not claimed, and Crashlytics delivery is outside this no-failure route probe.

## QA-BUG-0024 — Welcome foreground resume has no lifecycle observability

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; the Welcome route and local state restore correctly, but Analytics journey/session reporting cannot identify that the app left and re-entered the foreground, and diagnostic trails omit lifecycle context needed to explain restore behavior.
- **Status:** FIXED and post-commit retested on the Samsung. Original pre-fix failure and evidence are retained.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Welcome case 10; parameterized lifecycle case 741; screen-journey reconstruction case 747.
- **Device/build/commit:** Original failure: physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 build `S921BXXSGDZG1`; Parrot `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug, source commit `9958d084bf40bcf24024058c4d084fa04c3e1749`, APK SHA-256 `5c8fa064366ac9f03a642135e1e198babe8c9ad1e10415ff181a7296c2093adf`. Fixed/retested build: source commit `159fcb331ecd95ba9e154c5a510ae8bb6f008283`, same app version, APK SHA-256 `44529a4d0508ccb2965e4cb3cec5b5fa6ac8d2f3d35590fb91522a62565b2c63` (local and Samsung-installed package match).
- **Preconditions:** Root unauthenticated Welcome visible; Parrot process PID `22863`; normal foreground state and sensor rotation restored.
- **Exact reproduction:** Capture Welcome, send `KEYCODE_HOME`, wait five seconds, verify Samsung Launcher is top-resumed while the Parrot process remains alive, then bring `com.retro99.parrot/.android.MainActivity`'s existing task to front with `am start -W`. Inspect PID-scoped local Analytics and diagnostic logs.
- **Expected:** Welcome and its unchanged route/state are restored without a duplicate route-navigation event. Emit one bounded app-background and one app-foreground transition Analytics event with matching diagnostic breadcrumbs, so a later restoration failure can be correlated to the interruption. Rotation/configuration recreation must not be misreported as app background/foreground.
- **Actual:** Welcome was restored with the same content/actions and process/PID. There was no splash/login/blank flash or crash. No app lifecycle Analytics event, resumed Welcome view, or background/foreground breadcrumb appeared; only the original cold-start `welcome_screen_viewed` and its route-visible breadcrumb were present.
- **Frequency:** 1/1 Home→wait→resume run.
- **Evidence:** [case-10 pre-fix run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-010-welcome-home-resume-pre-fix.txt), [Welcome before](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-010-welcome-before-home.png), [Welcome after resume](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-010-welcome-after-resume.png). Launcher screenshot intentionally excluded because it contained unrelated personal notifications; top-resumed Launcher and retained Parrot PID are recorded in the run evidence.
- **Root cause:** `MainActivity` lifecycle callbacks coordinate OAuth, network callbacks and sync only; they emit no lifecycle Analytics or diagnostic signal. `LoginNavigation` observes destination-key changes but not app visibility transitions.
- **Affected files:** `androidApp/src/main/kotlin/com/retro99/parrot/android/MainActivity.kt`, `composeApp/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/parrot/navigation/RootNavigation.kt`/app lifecycle reporting, `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`, common lifecycle reporter tests and QA results.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Added `NavigationAnalyticsEvent.AppVisibilityChanged` with bounded app/lifecycle/operation/stage/outcome fields and `AppVisibilityReporter` to emit one Analytics event plus one diagnostic breadcrumb per true state transition. `MainActivity` reports on start/stop; configuration changes and finishing Activities are excluded. Added common transition/idempotence and Analytics sanitizer tests. Commit `159fcb331ecd95ba9e154c5a510ae8bb6f008283`.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung 2026-09-27. Home→5-second wait→existing-task resume retained the same app process PID `7573`, root Welcome route/content/actions and produced exactly one `background` event/breadcrumb and one `foreground` event/breadcrumb. No additional Welcome route exposure was emitted on resume. Portrait→landscape→portrait emitted no app-visibility transition; rotation settings were restored. No crash occurred. `:composeApp:testAndroidHostTest` (1 reporter test), `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest` (9 sanitizer tests) and `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed; installed/local APK hashes match (`44529a4d…65b2c63`). The iOS simulator test is environmentally blocked at link time because `FirebaseCore` is absent; it does not invalidate the Android run. Evidence: [post-fix case-10 retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-010-welcome-home-resume-retest.txt), original [pre-fix run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-010-welcome-home-resume-pre-fix.txt). Analytics is local debug-provider evidence only; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived, and Crashlytics delivery was not exercised by this normal-flow case.
- **QA evidence commit:** `454b678487e673f19936a04d1f72c9ae703b3218` records the Samsung retest, case result updates and privacy-safe screenshot selection.

## QA-BUG-0025 — Last-book launch lacks usable outcome telemetry and publication failure reporting

- **Severity / user impact:** Low for the working route; observability gap makes startup resume success/failure and recovery unmeasurable, and an unexpected publication-open failure can leave the user at Reader error without a Crashlytics report.
- **Status:** Instrumentation fix committed; Samsung case 11 runtime verification pending. The original gap was confirmed by source/schema audit before changes.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Startup case 11 (enabled), case 12 (enabled but no current book), extension cases 518–519 (missing/deleted target), Reader open/error cases 138, 166, 474, 485 and 612.
- **Device/build/commit:** Audit worktree HEAD `724c6f7d` with clean tree before this record; installed Samsung build is `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug, source commit `159fcb331ecd95ba9e154c5a510ae8bb6f008283`, APK SHA-256 `44529a4d0508ccb2965e4cb3cec5b5fa6ac8d2f3d35590fb91522a62565b2c63`; physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 build `S921BXXSGDZG1`.
- **Preconditions:** `OpenLastBookOnLaunch=true`, a valid currently-reading local fixture exists, and a process start creates `HomeNavigationViewModel`.
- **Exact reproduction / confirmation:** Source path `HomeNavigationViewModel.checkOpenLastBookOnLaunch()` logs `ContinueReadingLaunched(bookUuid)` before emitting `NavigateToReaderReplacing`. The analytics allowlist rejects `book_uuid`, so the event reaches the provider without useful parameters and denotes only a route request, not usable content. In `ReaderViewModel.openPublication()`, the publication-service failure emits `BookOpenFailed` and renders an error but does not call `AppError.log`; `initializeReader()` does report the earlier initialization failure. No bounded last-book success/failure/cancel outcome is currently joined to the launch route. This was verified from source; no case-11 UI sequence has yet been run.
- **Expected:** Bounded attempt at accepted last-book navigation; success only after usable Reader content is initialized; terminal failure/skipped/cancellation where applicable; sensitive book UUID/title/path omitted; unexpected exceptions reported once with typed reader operation context, while expected cancellation/validation is not reported.
- **Actual:** One identifier-only legacy event is attempted before navigation; provider sanitization strips its only parameter. No last-book usable-content terminal event exists. Publication-service failures have no exception reporter at that UI boundary.
- **Frequency:** Structural on each enabled startup with a current-book record; publication-service error reporting gap applies on its failure branch. Samsung frequency/observed counts pending case 11 and applicable failure variants.
- **Evidence:** [case-11 source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-last-book-launch-source-audit.txt); source references: `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt` (`checkOpenLastBookOnLaunch`), `AnalyticsParameterSanitizer.kt` (`SAFE_STRING_KEYS`), and `ReaderViewModel.kt` (`initializeReader` / `openPublication`).
- **Root cause:** Startup navigation telemetry is emitted at the route-request boundary with an identifier not approved by the provider schema. Reader open uses two failure boundaries, but only initialization invokes the existing error-reporting extension.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`, Home navigation route/event, `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/{ReaderScreen,ReaderViewModel}.kt`, `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`, sanitizer/tests, and QA inventory/results.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Added identifier-free `last_book_launch_attempted` and `last_book_launch_completed` events with bounded route/outcome/reason/type dimensions; mirrored attempt, terminal and retry breadcrumbs; propagated an app-launch marker into Reader; added a one-terminal-outcome-per-attempt gate; and routed Reader initialization/publication failures through one typed diagnostic boundary. Expected errors use outcome breadcrumbs without Crashlytics; only `shouldReportException` failures submit a non-fatal. Commit `ccd20066786a3ee178821fb699cd5aa9d2561266`.
- **Automated verification:** `:feature:reader:ui:testAndroidHostTest` passed (1 outcome-gate test); `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest` passed (10 sanitizer/schema tests); `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed. Built candidate APK SHA-256 `484505d137720fbef050c6b3c187b6148f6720404399c2613135ee32bba31ccf`. This artifact has not yet been installed on the Samsung at the time of this record.
- **Retest:** NOT RUN on device. Install/hash-match the committed build, then run cases 11–12 and applicable Reader startup recovery variants without clearing unrelated Parrot data. Firebase Analytics delivery is waived; Crashlytics delivery remains blocked by Console/settings access.

## QA-BUG-0026 — Abrupt process death left the auto-open target unset

- **Severity / user impact:** Confirmed moderate feature degradation/lost resume target; after a qualifying read followed by Recents swipe-away, the saved position remains locally visible but “Open last book on launch” has no current-book target and sends the user to Home instead of the Reader.
- **Status:** FIXED and Samsung-retested on 2026-09-27 for the Recents swipe-away variant. The pre-fix failure remains documented below and in its original evidence. Background-kill-with-task-retained and force-stop variants remain NOT RUN.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Startup case 11; process-death case 461; local Reader persistence cases 162–163 and 607.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 build `S921BXXSGDZG1`; installed debug `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), fix commit `b18cd671f5a54b504a1b39e6aa9e33918f25dfaa`, local/Samsung-pulled APK SHA-256 `2c5e6ec146c89fc8a4ebe8494a46949dd74104a18b38f4bc5556de25abf4b67b`. The prior failing build identity remains in [pre-fix evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-recents-swipe-process-death.txt).
- **Preconditions:** A controlled local EPUB is imported; clear the prior current-book reference using supported App Settings action; enable open-last-book; begin reading this EPUB long enough for the one-minute qualification; retain an observable saved page position.
- **Exact reproduction (pre-fix):** On 2026-09-27, with launch enabled and no current-book reference, enter the imported local *Pride and Prejudice* Reader at page 27/64 (48%), advance to page 28/64 (49%), and leave Reader active for about 90 seconds. Press Home, open Samsung Recents and swipe the Parrot task away while its Reader card is visible. `pidof` returned no process. Relaunch `MainActivity`; new PID `10521` resolved to Home and emitted `last_book_launch_completed` with `outcome=skipped`, `reason_code=no_current_book`. Home showed the saved library position at 49% but did not open Reader. This was a Recents swipe-away process death, not force-stop.
- **Expected:** Relaunch enters the just-read EPUB at its locally saved passage when the feature is enabled.
- **Actual:** Reader did not reopen. The page position was saved locally (visible as 49% in the Books card), but the current-reading target remained absent. The app safely landed on Home and emitted the no-current-book skip event/breadcrumb; those normal fallback signals do not identify that a just-active reading session lost its auto-open target.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix reproduction 1/1; final-build retest 1/1 restored the Reader. The earlier update-restart probe passed only because a target had already been persisted by a normal close; it was not evidence against this defect.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix case-11/case-461 Recents-swipe run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-recents-swipe-process-death.txt); [final-build retest with correlated checkpoint/startup logs and screenshots](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-recents-swipe-checkpoint-retest.txt); [case-11 source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-last-book-launch-source-audit.txt); `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/ReaderViewModel.kt`.
- **Root cause:** Before the fix, `CurrentlyReadingDomainModel` was written only inside `ReaderViewModel.close()` after `readingDurationMs >= 60_000`; `onCleared()` does not persist this target. Recents task removal bypassed the UI close action. Position checkpoints were persisted independently, explaining the visible 49% position alongside the absent auto-open target.
- **Affected files:** `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/ReaderViewModel.kt`, `CurrentBookTargetCheckpoint.kt`, `CurrentBookTargetPersistence.kt`, and `CurrentBookTargetSaveState.kt`; reader host regression tests for timer threshold/cancellation, persistence failure/retry, and duplicate-write state; `lib/analytics/api/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/analytics/api/AnalyticsEvent.kt`; Analytics sanitizer tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `b18cd671f5a54b504a1b39e6aa9e33918f25dfaa` (`fix(reader): checkpoint last-book target during reading [QA-BUG-0026, cases 11/461]`). After usable EPUB publication, a one-shot 60-second checkpoint writes the current-reading target without waiting for the UI close callback. Close cancels a pending checkpoint and retains the existing threshold fallback; successful target writes suppress duplicate close writes, while a failed checkpoint allows one later retry. Unexpected persistence exceptions are reported once at this operation boundary with a correlated bounded diagnostic context; cancellation is rethrown without a non-fatal. Typed Analytics distinguishes attempt, success, failure, entry point and retry without book identifiers/title/path.
- **Retest:** PASS on the final hash-matched Samsung APK `2c5e6ec1…abf4b67b`. Cleared the target through Settings while keeping launch enabled, read the local EPUB at page 28→29/64 (49%) for >60 seconds, observed one `current_book_target_save_attempted` + `current_book_target_save_completed` with a correlated start/success breadcrumb, swiped Parrot away from Recents, confirmed old PID disappeared, and relaunched into Reader at page 29/64 (49%) in new PID `31322`. Host tests passed: 7 Reader tests (2 checkpoint, 3 persistence, 2 save-state), 12 Analytics sanitizer tests; `:androidApp:assembleDebug` succeeded. The unexpected persistence-failure branch was unit-tested but not induced on-device; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived and Crashlytics delivery remains blocked/unverified. See [final retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-recents-swipe-checkpoint-retest.txt). Other case-461 interruption variants remain NOT RUN.

## QA-BUG-0027 — App Settings never exposes Clear current book

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate; users cannot clear the current-reading target from Settings, so they cannot reliably disable continue-reading state or meet the documented no-current-book startup scenario. Cases 12, 426 and 439 are blocked until the control state reflects stored state.
- **Status:** FIXED and Samsung-retested. Case 11's saved-position launch probe restored the book; the state observer reported `available`, the Settings row appeared, and clearing removed it. The distinct clear-outcome gap QA-BUG-0028 was also fixed and locally reverified. Case 12 now passes on the final QA-BUG-0028 APK; case 439 bubble behavior remains later in catalogue order.
- **Screen/test IDs:** App Settings case 426; startup case 12; continue-reading case 439; case-11 setup.
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, model SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 build `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug; pre-fix installed source `ccd20066786a3ee178821fb699cd5aa9d2561266`, APK SHA-256 `484505d137720fbef050c6b3c187b6148f6720404399c2613135ee32bba31ccf`. Fixed source commit `4277213608f77256b0ab922f18cf2a4c83ce589c`, package hash `b00fcb8c301412013aa1776399c3266749dd953a4ae85c64232505139ff6abd3`; Samsung-pulled and local APK hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** Guest/local Home, imported controlled Pride and Prejudice EPUB, “Open Last Book on Launch” enabled; Reader opened from Book Detail and remained active for more than 60 seconds before Back returned to Detail; App Settings reopened.
- **Exact reproduction:** On 2026-09-27, use the case-11 fixture, enter Settings, enable the launch preference, open *Pride and Prejudice*, read content, wait over one minute, press system Back, then open Settings. The Settings tree showed the preference checked but no “Clear current book” item between “Show Continue Reading” and “Reading statistics”; screenshot: [case-11 Settings observation](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-settings-clear-current-missing.png). No app data was cleared, and no account credentials were entered.
- **Expected:** When the stored currently-reading value exists, Settings displays “Clear current book”; the supported clear action gives its designed success confirmation/snackbar and removes the continue-reading target. The no-current precondition remains verifiable in the UI.
- **Actual (pre-fix):** Clear-current row was absent. `AppSettingsViewState.hasCurrentlyReadingBook` defaulted to `false`; `AppSettingsViewModel` did not observe the current-reading use case.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix, every App Settings ViewModel instance initialized the flag false and never updated it; observed once on Samsung after a qualifying Reader session. Whether the underlying target persisted was separately established by the subsequent launch probe.
- **Evidence:** Pre-fix [case-11/clear-current observation record](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-settings-clear-current-missing.txt) and [Samsung screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-settings-clear-current-missing.png). Post-fix [visible-row screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-settings-clear-current-fixed.png), [post-clear screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-result.png), [case-11 restart probe](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-011-update-restart-probe.txt), and [case-426 observation](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-observation.txt).
- **Root cause:** App Settings did not observe/initialize `hasCurrentlyReadingBook` from the current-reading data flow; the UI conditionally rendered the only clear action from that permanently-false state.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/appsettings/AppSettingsViewModel.kt`, `AppSettingsViewState.kt`, `feature/home/ui/src/commonTest/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/appsettings/AppSettingsViewStateTest.kt`, and related QA ledger rows for 12, 426 and 439.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `4277213608f77256b0ab922f18cf2a4c83ce589c` (`fix(settings): observe current book state [QA-BUG-0027, case 426]`). App Settings observes the active current-reading flow and maps null/non-null state; host regression test covers empty → present → cleared; structured start/state/failure breadcrumbs were added for observation.
- **Retest:** PASS for this functional defect on Samsung after installing the hash-matched APK without clearing app data: startup restored the current book, Settings showed “Clear Current Book,” and tapping it removed the row while both reading preferences remained enabled. Host state regression test (1) and `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed. Case 426's instrumentation was then repaired/retested by QA-BUG-0028 (`e54f99b8`). Case 12 passes on that final APK with the preference enabled and no current-book target. Clear cancellation is N-A because the action is immediate with a snackbar. Case 439 bubble behavior remains later in catalogue order.

## QA-BUG-0028 — Clear-current operation lacks attempt and diagnostic outcomes

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate instrumentation/recovery gap; reporting cannot distinguish a clear attempt from a completed or failed/retried operation. If persistence throws, the unguarded ViewModel action can escape without a user-facing recovery message or operation context; this failure impact is source-based and was not induced on device.
- **Status:** FIXED; final successful operation was retested on Samsung. The unexpected-storage-failure branch is covered by host unit tests but remains uninduced on-device because there is no safe preferences-write fault fixture. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics delivery remains blocked/unverified.
- **Screen/test IDs:** App Settings case 426; Home continue-reading case 439; startup no-current-book case 12.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug; final tested APK SHA-256 `2302df8024779c5f1cbff4e588965f1c1ec2fec7f15bddfde6a19412b8ed778e`, built from fix commit `e54f99b854b694d5b62db5338320afd75acdef35`; installed/local/pulled hashes match. Earlier pre-fix PID `10858` / APK `b00fcb8…ff6abd3` evidence remains in the original observation.
- **Preconditions:** Imported controlled local *Pride and Prejudice* fixture; current-book state available; “Open Last Book on Launch” and “Show Continue Reading” checked; App Settings visible.
- **Exact reproduction (pre-fix):** At about 01:41 CEST on 2026-09-27, tap “Clear Current Book.” The row disappears and the app emits the existing `current_book_cleared` Analytics event. The PID-scoped local log has no clear-attempt event and no clear-operation start/success/failure breadcrumb. `current_book_state` `started`/`available` breadcrumbs describe observer setup, not the user's clear operation. The pre-fix ViewModel invoked the use case and emitted only `CurrentBookCleared`, without an exception boundary or failed-operation outcome.
- **Expected:** One bounded clear attempt and a distinct terminal success/failure event; matching start/terminal breadcrumbs. Unexpected storage exceptions are reported once with typed context and an actionable retryable user message. Ordinary immediate completion has no cancellation path; a repeated user action after failure is counted as a new attempt, not an automatic retry.
- **Actual (pre-fix):** Exactly one `current_book_cleared` event appeared, without an attempt event, action breadcrumb or failure boundary. The successful action removed the row. No Firebase Analytics ingestion or Crashlytics delivery was claimed.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix gap reproduced in one successful Samsung action. Post-fix successful action produced one attempt + one terminal success and a correlated breadcrumb pair. Failure was not induced on device.
- **Evidence:** [pre-fix observation/log](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-observation.txt), [final-build Samsung retest/log](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-instrumented-retest.txt), [pre-clear Reader screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-instrumented-reader.png), and [post-clear screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-final-build.png). The older successful run is retained in [intermediate candidate evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-instrumented-success.png).
- **Root cause:** The pre-fix ViewModel treated any synchronous return as success and logged only a terminal success event; it had no attempt/failure taxonomy, diagnostic sequence or exception recovery.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/appsettings/AppSettingsViewModel.kt`, `AppSettingsViewState.kt`, `AppSettingsScreen.kt`, `AppSettingsIntent.kt`, `lib/analytics/api/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/analytics/api/BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`, translation resource files, and App Settings tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `e54f99b854b694d5b62db5338320afd75acdef35` (`fix(analytics): report clear-current outcomes [QA-BUG-0028, case 426]`). Adds bounded typed attempt/failure events and outcome parameters on the compatible `current_book_cleared` event; correlated start/terminal breadcrumbs; one UI-boundary unexpected-exception report; preserves the row and presents retry guidance on failure. Ordinary cancellation is rethrown and not reported as a failure.
- **Retest:** PASS on the Samsung for successful clear: one `current_book_clear_attempted`, one `current_book_cleared` with `outcome=succeeded`, `is_retry=false`, and a matching start/success breadcrumb correlation ID; row removed, both preferences unchanged. `:feature:home:ui:testAndroidHostTest` passes 5 tests including success/failure/retry/state/cancellation; `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest` passes 11 sanitizer tests; Android debug assembly succeeds. Unit tests verify a thrown storage exception is reported once with bounded context and retry succeeds. On-device failure injection and Crashlytics receipt remain BLOCKED; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived. See [final-build retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-426-clear-current-instrumented-retest.txt).

## QA-BUG-0029 — Retained-task process death restores a black Reader surface

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate recoverable degradation; retained-task restoration left the core reading surface blank despite a live activity, saved position and apparently successful startup. Manual recovery requires Back to Book Detail and Continue reading.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the Samsung on 2026-09-27 for the `am kill` + retained-Recents-task variant. The original black-surface failure and intermediate candidate outcomes remain preserved below; final candidate restored visible content and emitted one successful terminal outcome. Do not merge this defect with QA-BUG-0026; the latter's task-swipe-away cold relaunch is a distinct variant.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Reader; shared lifecycle/process-death case 461; startup case 11 and Reader restoration case 607.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug; QA-BUG-0026 build commit `b18cd671f5a54b504a1b39e6aa9e33918f25dfaa`, APK SHA-256 `2c5e6ec146c89fc8a4ebe8494a46949dd74104a18b38f4bc5556de25abf4b67b` (local and Samsung-pulled match).
- **Preconditions:** Default profile; valid imported local *Pride and Prejudice*; current-book target available; Reader at page 29/64 (49%); process task can remain in Recents.
- **Exact reproduction:** At about 03:13 CEST, leave Reader open at page 29/64 and wait for the current-book target checkpoint. Press Home, open Recents, confirm Parrot task exists, then run `adb -s RFCWC0SSVDM shell am kill com.retro99.parrot` (a direct `kill -9 <pid>` was denied by Android shell). Confirm `pidof` is empty but task card remains. Tap Parrot in Recents. New PID `8228` returns to Reader with title/toolbar and 49% indicator but all publication content black. Wait >30 seconds and tap content; toolbar toggles but page remains black.
- **Expected:** Recents task restoration should show the saved passage/page or a bounded recoverable error; it must not present blank content as a successful usable Reader.
- **Actual:** Solid black publication area persisted >30 seconds; toolbar/title and 49% indicator were present after task launch, but no book text/content. A local `last_book_launch_completed` event and breadcrumb reported `stage=usable_content`, `outcome=succeeded` at 03:14:34.885, while the screen remained blank. Manual system Back to Detail followed by tapping Continue reading recovered page 29/64 in the same process. No fatal marker or actionable blank-render diagnostic was found in the inspected logs.
- **Frequency:** Reproduced in 1/1 retained-task process-kill attempts. A distinct Recents swipe-away followed by launching MainActivity did restore the EPUB successfully (QA-BUG-0026 retest); mechanisms must remain separate.
- **Evidence:** [Exact case-461 retained-task run and local logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-retained-task-process-kill.txt); pre-kill [Reader screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-reader-before-background-kill.png); post-kill [black Reader screenshots](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-after-retained-task-kill.png), [after tap](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-after-retained-task-tap.png), and [settled](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-after-retained-task-settled.png); [manual exit/reopen recovery](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-retained-task-manual-recovery.txt) and [recovered-content screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-manual-recovery-content.png).
- **Root cause:** Confirmed conflicting restoration paths: `HomeNavigationStateHolder` saves the per-tab Reader back stack across process death, while `HomeNavigationViewModel.checkOpenLastBookOnLaunch()` independently emitted a replacement Reader route for the same current book. `HomeNavigation` consumed it with unconditional `navigateToReplacing()`, resulting in duplicate Reader initialization with the same book-keyed Fragment/controller. The saved Reader destination now wins; startup recognizes a matching route before emitting a replacement. This avoids both duplicate opens and the black retained-task surface.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationViewModel.kt` (startup auto-open decision), `HomeNavigation.kt` (restored-stack-aware consumption), `HomeNavigationStateHolder.kt` (saved Reader route), `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/ReaderViewModel.kt` (one initialization/event per reader session), and Android `EpubReaderView.android.kt` (same per-book Fragment tag).
- **Fix reference / commit:** `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98` (`fix(navigation): reuse restored Reader launch [QA-BUG-0029/0030/0031, case 461]`). The saved-route guard and single-owner outcome handling prevent duplicate Reader initialization while preserving the Reader-owned terminal outcome.
- **Retest:** PASS on the final-hash candidate APK `d3770e18814e974053ece48ba03e2058bb212762675ac7fe37178124731dba47`: after `am kill` with task retained, tapping Parrot in Recents restored visible text at page 29/64 (49%) in new PID `2107`. Local logs showed exactly one last-book attempt, one `book_opened`, one `last_book_launch_completed(outcome=succeeded)`, a non-terminal route-restored breadcrumb, and the matching success breadcrumb. No skipped terminal was emitted. Original FAIL (PID `8228`), intermediate duplicate-terminal run (PID `26622`) and manual Back → Continue recovery are retained. See [final retained-task retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-restored-route-outcome-fix-retest.txt). Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics delivery remains blocked/unverified.
- **Discovery/documentation commits:** initial failure/evidence `3f5493c868cf5374e5f4cef6ff2468b5a51a1e5c`; recovery/duplicate-open evidence `e9570ee0939f4d64297d16182aa579c0960827c6`; source audit `ea98eeaa883c3d23a0519fd8958d28b8f81d16f2`; intermediate candidate and QA-BUG-0031 discovery `894706d1`; final retest/evidence and fix `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98`.

## QA-BUG-0030 — Automatic retained-task Reader restore logs book-opened twice

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate Analytics integrity gap; one automatic Reader restoration appears as two `book_opened` uses, inflating open counts and success denominators. The same startup also emitted the last-book terminal event once.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the Samsung on 2026-09-27. The pre-fix retained-task startup emitted two `book_opened` events 10 ms apart; the final restoration-aware build emitted exactly one event for the single rendered Reader session. Do not treat the original duplicate as a separate user tap.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Reader case 138; startup case 11; retained-task lifecycle case 461.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug; source commit `b18cd671f5a54b504a1b39e6aa9e33918f25dfaa`; APK SHA-256 `2c5e6ec146c89fc8a4ebe8494a46949dd74104a18b38f4bc5556de25abf4b67b`.
- **Preconditions:** Current-book target available; Reader active at page 29/64; Samsung Recents task retained; process terminated with `am kill` and task reopened.
- **Exact reproduction:** In PID `8228`, after tapping the retained Parrot Recents task at about 03:14:34 CEST, inspect local DebugAnalyticsManager output: `book_opened` at `03:14:34.874` and again at `03:14:34.884`, then one `last_book_launch_completed` at `03:14:34.885`. No user input to open a second book occurred between them. Later manual Back → Continue reading at 03:20:15 emitted one `book_opened`.
- **Expected:** One committed `book_opened` event per successfully rendered/opened book session; restoration or recomposition must not double-count one startup. The success metric should be emitted only when content is actually usable.
- **Actual:** Two `book_opened` events occurred within 10 ms for one automatic restoration; simultaneously the content was black, and the last-book event incorrectly declared usable content. QA-BUG-0029 tracks the blank surface/readiness gap separately unless source tracing proves a coupled root cause.
- **Frequency:** Observed 2/2 events during one automatic retained-task launch (1 startup); manual retry recovery emitted one event in one follow-up opening. Additional startup variants not yet repeated.
- **Evidence:** [Retained-task and duplicate event log](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-retained-task-process-kill.txt), [manual recovery and comparison event](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-retained-task-manual-recovery.txt).
- **Root cause:** Same confirmed conflicting saved-route/auto-open path as QA-BUG-0029: two Reader initializations invoked the single `ReaderViewModel.openPublication()` analytics call site twice. The source now recognizes and retains a matching restored route instead of creating another Reader; no time-window dedupe was added.
- **Affected files:** `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt` and `HomeNavigation.kt` for restored-route-aware auto-open; `HomeNavigationState.kt` plus common tests for matching the current book; Reader event tests/QA result ledger for exactly-once verification.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98` (`fix(navigation): reuse restored Reader launch [QA-BUG-0029/0030/0031, case 461]`); same coupled route-restoration fix as QA-BUG-0029.
- **Retest:** PASS on final candidate PID `2107`: restored page 29/64 (49%), exactly one `book_opened` and one successful last-book terminal; no duplicate or contradictory outcomes. Earlier intermediate PASS-for-event/FAIL-for-terminal run PID `26622` remains retained in [candidate evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-route-guard-retained-task-candidate.txt); initial duplicate in PID `8228` is preserved. See [final retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-restored-route-outcome-fix-retest.txt). Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; this is local DebugAnalyticsManager evidence only.
- **Discovery/documentation commits:** `e9570ee0939f4d64297d16182aa579c0960827c6` (initial finding), `ea98eeaa883c3d23a0519fd8958d28b8f81d16f2` (source audit), `894706d1` (intermediate candidate observation), and `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98` (final retest/evidence and fix).

## QA-BUG-0031 — Restored Reader route emits two last-book terminal outcomes

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate Analytics/diagnostics integrity gap; a single restored Reader task is recorded as both skipped and successfully opened, making terminal funnels contradictory and double-counting outcomes.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the Samsung on 2026-09-27. The intermediate candidate emitted contradictory `skipped/reader_route_restored` and `succeeded/usable_content` outcomes; the final build leaves terminal ownership with the restored auto-launch Reader and emits one success.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Startup case 11; process-death/restoration case 461; Home navigation and Reader.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug source commit `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98`; APK SHA-256 `d3770e18814e974053ece48ba03e2058bb212762675ac7fe37178124731dba47` (local and Samsung-pulled match).
- **Preconditions:** Current-book target is the same EBOOK as the saved top Reader destination; saved Reader has `isLastBookOnLaunch=true`; Parrot process is killed while its task remains in Recents.
- **Exact reproduction:** With candidate APK, PID `25636` first opened the book normally. Pressed Home → Recents, ran `adb -s RFCWC0SSVDM shell am kill com.retro99.parrot`, then tapped the retained Parrot card. New PID `26622` restored page 29/64 at 49%. Its local logs at 03:42:15.746 reported `last_book_launch_completed outcome=skipped reason_code=reader_route_restored`; at 03:42:16.005, the same PID reported `last_book_launch_completed stage=usable_content outcome=succeeded`. One `book_opened` event appeared at 03:42:16.003.
- **Expected:** Exactly one terminal last-book outcome per accepted launch/restoration attempt. If the restored Reader owns the launch, only its eventual success/failure/cancellation should resolve it; navigation must not emit an additional terminal skip.
- **Actual:** Intermediate candidate: contradictory skipped and succeeded terminal events. Fixed candidate: one attempt, one non-terminal restored-route breadcrumb, one `book_opened`, one succeeded terminal and matching breadcrumb for a single rendered Reader.
- **Frequency:** Intermediate defect reproduced in 1/1 candidate retained-task restore (2 terminal events / 1 startup); final corrected outcome observed in 1/1 retained-task retest (one terminal / one startup).
- **Evidence:** [Intermediate contradictory outcome](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-route-guard-retained-task-candidate.txt); [final corrected outcome, screenshots and PID-scoped logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-461-restored-route-outcome-fix-retest.txt).
- **Root cause:** The first route-restoration fix logged `skipped/reader_route_restored` whenever a matching Reader existed, without respecting the persisted `isLastBookOnLaunch` ownership flag. The ReaderViewModel then correctly resolved its own success. The final fix leaves terminal resolution to a matching restored auto-launch Reader; a matching ordinary restored Reader still records a bounded skip because no Reader launch terminal owner exists.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`, Home route consumption in `HomeNavigation.kt`, restored destination matching, and Analytics/navigation unit tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98` (`fix(navigation): reuse restored Reader launch [QA-BUG-0029/0030/0031, case 461]`). The code preserves the ReaderViewModel's terminal owner when a matching saved Reader has `isLastBookOnLaunch=true`; a matching ordinary restored Reader with no pending launch outcome gets one explicit skipped terminal. No time-based event deduplication.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung final-hash candidate APK `d3770e18814e974053ece48ba03e2058bb212762675ac7fe37178124731dba47`, PID `2107`: exactly one `last_book_launch_attempted`, one `book_opened`, one `last_book_launch_completed(outcome=succeeded, stage=usable_content)`, and a non-terminal route-restored breadcrumb followed by the success breadcrumb. EPUB text visible at page 29/64, 49%. Host route tests (2), Analytics sanitizer tests (12) and Android debug assembly passed. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics delivery remains unverified.
- **Discovery/evidence commits:** intermediate QA-BUG-0031 observation `894706d1`; final fix/retest/evidence `13ead5d85f5db281805fb52dcc35a0706a1d6e98`.

## QA-BUG-0032 — Settings tab appeared selected without leaving Statistics (false positive)

- **Severity / user impact:** None; initial apparent mismatch is explained by the intended independent Settings-tab back stack. No Settings action is blocked.
- **Status:** CLOSED — NOT A BUG after source audit and Samsung reproduction on 2026-09-27. Preserve the initial confirmed-looking observation and original evidence; later stack tracing established the Statistics route was valid content on the Settings tab. No application fix was needed.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Main tab navigation, Statistics, Settings; case 535 (Statistics → Settings pair, partial variant), with context from cases 385 and 695.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source commit `c08658a278fcef1da884352edb44ffc298d6b08d`, APK SHA-256 `df2a162c85b7b7b32c7c7d58608733e957fc3d42d677d69fe402b2caa4a5c506`, PID `10613`. Original false-positive observation remains separately tied to source commit `13ead5d8`, APK `d3770e18…31dba47`, PID `20992`.
- **Preconditions:** Statistics dashboard visible in the default profile, airplane mode off, app data and local library unchanged.
- **Exact reproduction:** From Statistics, tap bottom-navigation `Settings` three times after waiting for each UI to settle (about 04:24:06, 04:24:20 and 04:24:32 CEST). After each, inspect the title/body and selected-tab styling.
- **Expected:** The selected tab displays the top destination on that tab's own saved back stack; when the Settings stack is at its root, show the Settings hub.
- **Initial actual observation:** Settings gear was selected while the title remained `Reading Statistics`. Three `tab_switched {tab_name=settings}` records were seen. This was first misread as a failed route transition.
- **Frequency:** Reproduced as expected on the saved Settings stack. From the Settings hub, its Reading statistics row pushes `HomeDestination.Statistics` onto the Settings stack; switching away and returning correctly restores Statistics. Back returns to the Settings hub. Samsung confirmed on commit `13ead5d8`, PID `20992`.
- **Evidence:** [Original observation and logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/qa-bug-0032-settings-tab-no-op-observation.txt), [initial screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-014-pre-offline-statistics-state.png), and [follow-up source/stack verification](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/qa-bug-0032-reclassification-follow-up.txt) with [case-535 screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-535-statistics-settings-retained-stack.png).
- **Root cause:** Expected independent-stack behavior, not a defect. `HomeTab.Settings.startDestination` is `AppSettings`; Settings' Reading statistics row pushes `HomeDestination.Statistics` onto the Settings stack. Returning to Settings restores the top Statistics route, which is why Statistics remains visible while Settings is selected. The Back affordance returns to the hub.
- **Affected files:** None; audited `HomeTab.kt`, `HomeNavigation.kt`, and `HomeNavigationStateHolder.kt`; no source change.
- **Fix reference / commit:** None; closed as expected behavior. The incomplete `tab_switched` source/entry semantics are tracked separately as QA-BUG-0033.
- **Retest:** PASS for the exercised Settings-stack route and Statistics ↔ Settings tab pair on Samsung: selected tab corresponded to the active stack; Statistics was restored when appropriate; system Back returned to the Settings hub. This is partial case 535 evidence, not an all-tab-pairs sign-off. Firebase Analytics delivery is waived; Crashlytics was not applicable because no failure occurred.
- **Discovery/reclassification commits:** Original observation recorded in `c8a8f2ab`; expected stack behavior and QA-BUG-0033 source finding recorded in `97345756`.

## QA-BUG-0033 — Tab-switch Analytics omits source and entry point

- **Severity / user impact:** Moderate observability gap; local event data identifies only the destination tab, so it cannot reconstruct where the user came from or distinguish a real switch from reselecting the already active tab. This weakens tab adoption, journey and outcome analysis but does not block navigation.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on Samsung on 2026-09-27 for the exercised Books/Settings/Statistics transitions and Settings reselect. The all-tab-pairs screen pass remains incomplete.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Main tab navigation; cases 535 (all ordered tab pairs) and 536 (reselect current tab), plus case 469's retained independent tab stacks.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), debug source commit `c08658a278fcef1da884352edb44ffc298d6b08d`, APK SHA-256 `df2a162c85b7b7b32c7c7d58608733e957fc3d42d677d69fe402b2caa4a5c506`, PID `10613`. The initial false-positive observation remains separately tied to commit `13ead5d8`, APK `d3770e18…31dba47`, PID `20992`.
- **Preconditions:** Default profile, Settings tab's back stack contains Statistics pushed from the Settings hub; current device locale `en-GB`; local debug analytics provider enabled.
- **Exact reproduction:** From Settings hub, open its Reading statistics row. Select the Statistics tab, then Settings; confirm the Statistics route is restored on Settings. Press system Back and confirm Settings hub. Separately reselect Settings while already selected. Inspect PID-scoped logs.
- **Expected:** For actual tab transitions, bounded Analytics identifies source tab, destination tab, and `bottom_navigation` entry point after the navigation is applied. A reselect must not be labeled `tab_switched`; if useful, report it as a distinct reselect/action. Firebase delivery remains a separate verification.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix events contained only `tab_name`; they omitted source, entry point and outcome. Three taps on the already selected Settings tab at 04:24 emitted three `tab_switched {tab_name=settings}` events. Fixed build emits one `tab_switch_attempted` with source/destination/entry point, followed by one `tab_switched(outcome=succeeded)` only after application; same-tab selection emits a distinct `tab_reselected`.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix 3/3 observed reselects were mislabeled as switches and all old events lacked source. Fixed Samsung retest observed 7 actual transitions with 7 attempts + 7 successful terminals, and one reselect with only one `tab_reselected`.
- **Evidence:** [Final PID-scoped logs, host checks and Samsung outcomes](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-535-536-tab-analytics-fix-retest.txt), [Settings and retained-route screenshots](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-535-tab-analytics-final-settings-hub.png) / [`Statistics` retained on Settings](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-535-tab-analytics-final-retained-stats.png), and [reselect screenshot](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-536-tab-reselect-final.png). Pre-fix source evidence remains [here](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/qa-bug-0032-reclassification-follow-up.txt).
- **Root cause:** Confirmed in pre-fix source: `HomeNavigationViewModel.onIntent(SwitchTab)` received only the destination tab, logged `TabSwitched(tabName)` before the navigation owner applied the event, and could not distinguish a real change from selecting the current tab. `NavigationAnalyticsEvent.TabSwitched` serialized only `tab_name`.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationIntent.kt`, `HomeNavigation.kt`, `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`; `lib/analytics/api/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/analytics/api/BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`; Analytics and navigation tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `c08658a278fcef1da884352edb44ffc298d6b08d` (`fix(analytics): attribute tab navigation outcomes [QA-BUG-0033, cases 535/536]`). Source/data tests add bounded source/destination/entry-point dimensions, separate attempt from applied success/failure, report same-tab selection separately, and correlate started/terminal diagnostic breadcrumbs without forwarding the ID to Analytics.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung APK SHA-256 `df2a162c85b7b7b32c7c7d58608733e957fc3d42d677d69fe402b2caa4a5c506`, PID `10613`: seven actual switches in the full PID log each had one attempt and one successful outcome with source/destination/entry point; their started/terminal breadcrumbs shared diagnostic-only correlation IDs. One reselect emitted only `tab_reselected`. No fatal or switch failure occurred. Analytics sanitizer host tests, Home UI host tests, and `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics was N-A for this successful navigation run and delivery remains unverified.
- **Discovery/fix/documentation commits:** source gap and QA-BUG-0032 correction recorded in `97345756`; focused fix in `c08658a278fcef1da884352edb44ffc298d6b08d`; retest/build-hash bookkeeping is in this documentation follow-up.

## QA-BUG-0034 — Login server-type picker usage is not instrumented

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; no functional blockage observed, but server-type picker adoption, selection and abandonment cannot be measured or diagnosed from app telemetry.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on Samsung for both selectable types and case-751 outside/System-Back dismissals. Pre-fix observation and gap are preserved below.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Login/server type picker; case 15 (Storyteller and Audiobookshelf choices), case 751 (dismissal variants), and shared Login screen open/dismiss requirements.
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; pre-fix source `c08658a278fcef1da884352edb44ffc298d6b08d`, APK `df2a162c…a5c506`, PID `21574`; fixed source `d69be066b7b864d8c8d4c15a111132eaa17e1db9`, debug APK SHA-256 `1b3f3480c7e33852d5b3e07e95e719051c776950098d932ac710677f322d0197`, PID `5883`, package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21).
- **Preconditions:** Login opened from Settings → Servers → Add Server; clean Login form, default Storyteller server type, local DebugAnalyticsManager active; no credentials entered.
- **Exact reproduction:** At about 05:16 CEST tap the Server Type field, verify the menu contains Storyteller and Audiobookshelf only, then tap Audiobookshelf. Inspect the selected form value and PID-scoped Analytics/breadcrumb logs.
- **Expected:** Log one bounded picker attempt/open with current server type; after applying a choice, log one typed successful selection with previous/new server type and a matching diagnostic terminal breadcrumb. When dismissed without a choice (case 751), log cancellation and a terminal breadcrumb, with no selection/success event. No URL, username, password or other free-form input is included.
- **Actual:** The two required choices render; selecting Audiobookshelf closes the menu and changes the form value. Only `login_screen_viewed` and its screen-visible breadcrumb were emitted; picker open and selection produced no telemetry. Source `handleServerTypeSelected` updates state and validation only; no picker open/dismiss intents or server-type picker events exist in the Analytics API.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix gap confirmed in 1/1 Samsung selection attempt: zero picker event/breadcrumbs for opening or selecting. Fixed committed-build retest: 4/4 interactions (two selections, outside dismiss and system-Back dismiss) each had exactly one attempt plus one matching success/cancel terminal and correlated diagnostic start/terminal breadcrumbs.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix exact steps and local event audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-server-type-picker-prefx.txt), [pre-fix menu](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-server-type-picker-prefx-open.png), [pre-fix selected type](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-server-type-picker-prefx-selected.png), and [committed-build Samsung retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-server-type-picker-retest.txt) with [menu](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-committed-menu-options.png), [Audiobookshelf selection](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-committed-audiobookshelf-selected.png), [Storyteller selection](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-015-committed-storyteller-selected.png), [outside dismissal](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-751-committed-outside-dismissal.png) and [system Back](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-751-committed-system-back-dismissal.png).
- **Root cause:** The dropdown stores expansion in local Compose state, with `onDismissRequest` only closing it; selection intent reaches a ViewModel handler that changes `selectedServerType` without analytics/diagnostics. No typed picker event exists, and the sanitizer has no previous-server-type key.
- **Affected files:** `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/login/LoginScreen.kt`, `LoginIntent.kt`, `LoginViewModel.kt`, `feature/login/ui/build.gradle.kts`, `LoginServerTypePickerTest.kt`; `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`; Analytics sanitizer/tests; case 15/751 records.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `d69be066b7b864d8c8d4c15a111132eaa17e1db9` (`fix(analytics): instrument Login server picker [QA-BUG-0034]`). Adds typed attempted/selected/cancelled events, bounded server type dimensions, picker intents, diagnostic breadcrumbs sharing a diagnostic-only correlation ID, and Login UI/sanitizer regression tests. Expected picker cancellation does not report to Crashlytics.
- **Retest:** PASS on exact local/Samsung APK SHA-256 `1b3f3480c7e33852d5b3e07e95e719051c776950098d932ac710677f322d0197`, PID `5883`: only Storyteller/Audiobookshelf were offered; each updated the selected form type and produced one attempt + one success with a correlated breadcrumb pair. Outside tap and system Back each left Login unchanged, emitted one attempt + cancellation, and no selection event; both cancellations use `reason_code=dismiss_request`. No fatal/unexpected exception occurred. Host tests `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest` and `:feature:login:ui:testAndroidHostTest` passed; `:androidApp:assembleDebug` succeeded. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; DebugAnalyticsManager proves local production only. Crashlytics is N-A for normal choices/cancellations and delivery remains unverified.
- **Discovery/fix/documentation commits:** pre-fix gap and screenshots in `7a35f9425b898dc74a94440049d6d40ba1e52191`; focused instrumentation/test fix `d69be066b7b864d8c8d4c15a111132eaa17e1db9`; committed-build retest/results/evidence recorded in this documentation follow-up.

## QA-BUG-0035 — Login URL-help tooltip usage is not instrumented

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; help content works, but users' help opens and dismissals cannot be measured or correlated with Login interactions.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED for case 16 on the committed Samsung build. The first candidate's missing `Got it` terminal outcome is retained as failed-history evidence below. Case 752 outside/System-Back dismissal variants remain NOT RUN until their catalogue position.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Login URL help; case 16 and appended case 752 (button, outside tap, system Back where supported).
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; original pre-fix `d69be066b7b864d8c8d4c15a111132eaa17e1db9` / APK `1b3f3480…f322d0197` / PID `5883`; first candidate `3b2b5d3d5e80ee30e62551a4946891971ee355bc` / APK `792269343f30a51198367cc7a8a22419e325ce48daa0e7a38d432688010d3431` / PID `25002`; corrected committed build `0eaa3ec6a6945199bbd80d765e6efa9ef4aede0e` / APK `75425b0b0d4fe71eecccfbb89aa12bfd9caf1023c63883d2100f2cba2c08a196` / PID `28181`; all package version 0.4.5 (21).
- **Preconditions:** Login opened from Settings → Servers → Add Server; Storyteller selected; URL placeholder only; username/password empty; local DebugAnalyticsManager active.
- **Exact reproduction:** Tap the accessible `URL info` help icon next to Server URL. Confirm the `Server URL` RichTooltip appears with full-scheme/local-address/domain guidance; tap `Got it` to dismiss. Inspect form state and PID-scoped Analytics/diagnostic log.
- **Expected:** One bounded help-open attempt and opened success with a matching diagnostic start/terminal breadcrumb and diagnostic-only correlation ID; one distinct dismissal outcome for the close action, without any login success/failure event. No URL, username, password or tooltip/free-form text should be sent. Expected dismissal does not warrant a Crashlytics issue.
- **Actual:** Tooltip opens, displays the guidance and `Got it` closes it without changing the form. No tooltip-specific Analytics event or diagnostic breadcrumb is emitted. `UrlInfoTooltip` handles the interaction entirely in local Compose state, without callbacks/intents to the Login ViewModel.
- **Frequency:** Initial pre-fix Samsung run: 1/1 help open and `Got it` dismissal produced zero tooltip events/breadcrumbs. First candidate: 1/1 attempt/opened pair and start/visible breadcrumb pair, but zero Got it terminal. Corrected committed build: 1/1 help-open operation produced exactly one attempt, one opened success, one Got it cancellation and three correlated breadcrumbs.
- **Evidence:** [Initial pre-fix steps and log/source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-prefx.txt), [pre-fix closed form](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-prefx-closed.png), [pre-fix open tooltip](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-prefx-open.png), [pre-fix dismissed form](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-prefx-dismissed.png), [first-fix failed Samsung retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-first-fix-retest.txt) with [candidate open](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-fixed-open.png) and [candidate Got it dismissal](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-fixed-got-it-dismissed.png), and [corrected committed-build retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-corrected-retest.txt) with [tooltip open](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-corrected-open.png) and [after Got it](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-016-url-tooltip-corrected-got-it-dismissed.png).
- **Root cause:** `LoginScreen.kt`'s private `UrlInfoTooltip` owns `rememberTooltipState` and invokes `show`/`dismiss` directly; no event type, Login intent, ViewModel operation, or diagnostic context represents help use.
- **Affected files (planned):** `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/login/LoginScreen.kt`, `LoginIntent.kt`, `LoginViewModel.kt`; `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`, Analytics sanitizer/tests, Login host tests; case 16/752 evidence and results.
- **Fix reference / commits:** Initial instrumentation and regression tests: `3b2b5d3d5e80ee30e62551a4946891971ee355bc` (`fix(analytics): instrument Login URL help [QA-BUG-0035]`). Its first Samsung retest found the omitted Got it dispatch. Corrective callback: `0eaa3ec6a6945199bbd80d765e6efa9ef4aede0e` (`fix(analytics): report URL help dismissal [QA-BUG-0035, case 16]`). The second commit dispatches the explicit bounded `got_it` cancellation intent before dismissing the tooltip.
- **Retest:** Corrected build PASS on exact local/Samsung APK SHA-256 `75425b0b0d4fe71eecccfbb89aa12bfd9caf1023c63883d2100f2cba2c08a196`, PID `28181`: accessible URL-help opens and shows guidance; Got it closes it and leaves Login form unchanged. Exactly one attempt, opened success and `reason_code=got_it` cancellation event plus matching correlated started/visible/dismissed breadcrumbs. No auth outcome, free-form form data, fatal or unexpected exception. `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest`, `:feature:login:ui:testAndroidHostTest`, and `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; local DebugAnalyticsManager only is evidenced. Crashlytics is N-A for normal use and delivery remains unverified. Case 752 outside/System-Back variants remain NOT RUN and are separate.
- **Discovery/documentation commits:** Original gap/pre-fix evidence in `2d93e9f95bfd14edbb7e1bb67b408ac6cb6a00a0`; first failed retest and omitted callback in `f4cc20481d8604b651521350c81bd0bc39d30cdb`; corrected-build evidence/results and commit bookkeeping are added in this documentation follow-up.

## QA-BUG-0036 — Invalid Login URL validation outcome is not instrumented

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; inline validation works and prevents submission, but invalid-URL form outcomes are not measurable or diagnosable from the app's bounded telemetry.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the corrected committed Samsung build. Preserve the original gap and first candidate's duplicate-event failure below as history.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Login credentials form; case 17.
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; pre-fix build source `0eaa3ec6a6945199bbd80d765e6efa9ef4aede0e`, APK `75425b0b0d4fe71eecccfbb89aa12bfd9caf1023c63883d2100f2cba2c08a196`, PID `28181`; first candidate from uncommitted tree atop `33a702168e5845bb126ee40e2c399b353d4de7d7`, APK `3b08fdf1adce4bc84b1df811279cc5e2071068f7ed506138ba9dcf3213300b95`, PID `6557`; corrected committed source `edebeb69c0f254888870b7209ad124b1ab5fd0c1`, APK `ad132c11b257d5ae8294d932319e38643b941ef772df0b9988297ce2024d2e85`, PID `9237`; regression build `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc`, APK `99804cc1ef26d7f1deeb65de3ff359c90481c8034f99c7edadb08c8396e58450`, PID `22587`. All package version 0.4.5 (21).
- **Preconditions:** Login opened from Settings → Servers → Add Server; Storyteller selected; URL placeholder `https://`; username/password empty; no credentials supplied.
- **Exact reproduction:** Replace URL with `not a url`; observe the inline URL error and disabled Sign In button; attempt to tap Sign In; inspect PID-scoped Analytics, diagnostic and fatal logs.
- **Expected:** Invalid URL is surfaced inline and submission stays blocked. Emit one bounded validation-failed Analytics outcome and diagnostic breadcrumb for the transition into the invalid URL state (not one per keystroke). Analytics carries only `screen=login`, action/operation, bounded `field=server_url`, `reason_code=invalid_url` and safe server type; the breadcrumb carries bounded screen/action/operation/reason/server type plus diagnostic-only correlation ID. Do not emit login attempt/failure because submission was not accepted. No raw URL, username or password; no Crashlytics non-fatal for ordinary validation.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix, the inline `Enter a valid http(s) URL` error appeared and Sign In was disabled/no-op, but no validation event/breadcrumb was emitted. The first candidate emitted duplicates because deleting the placeholder passed through a blank state. The corrected `edebeb69` build emitted exactly one bounded validation event/breadcrumb on invalid entry. The later QA-BUG-0037 regression build keeps the URL error, permits submit-time validation, displays missing username/password errors as well, and emits no extra validation event on submit; no request, spinner, crash or login attempt occurs.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix: 1/1 invalid entry had zero validation events/breadcrumbs. First candidate: 1/1 replacement produced two events and two breadcrumbs. Corrected `edebeb69` build: 1/1 replacement produced exactly one event and one breadcrumb; disabled-button tap added none. Regression build `24973eb0`: 1/1 invalid edit produced exactly one `server_url` event/breadcrumb; tapping Sign In added no duplicate/auth attempt. Process remained alive throughout.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix run, source and log audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-prefx.txt), [pre-fix invalid input](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-entered.png), [pre-fix disabled Sign In tap](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-submit-attempt.png), [first candidate failed retest/logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-first-fix-retest.txt) with [candidate input](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-fixed-entered.png) and [disabled tap](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-candidate-submit.png), [corrected committed-build retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-corrected-retest.txt), and [required-field fix regression retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-empty-submit-regression.txt) with [invalid input](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-empty-submit-fix-entered.png) and [after submit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-empty-submit-fix-tapped.png).
- **Pre-fix root cause:** The `snapshotFlow` of the text fields called `updateFormState`, which validated and published `urlError` but did not report the newly-entered validation state through Analytics or diagnostics. There is no submit intent because the button is disabled.
- **Affected files:** `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/login/LoginValidationTelemetry.kt`, `LoginViewModel.kt`, Login host tests, `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt`, analytics parameter sanitizer/tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `edebeb69c0f254888870b7209ad124b1ab5fd0c1` (`fix(analytics): report invalid login URL [QA-BUG-0036, case 17]`). The reporter latches across transient blank/placeholder text and resets only after a syntactically valid server URL; it emits bounded `field=server_url` and `reason_code=invalid_url` dimensions without an exception or per-keystroke duplicate.
- **Retest:** PASS on original corrected commit `edebeb69…`, and regression PASS on source `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc`, APK SHA-256 `99804cc1ef26d7f1deeb65de3ff359c90481c8034f99c7edadb08c8396e58450`, PID `22587`. On the regression build, URL invalidation emitted exactly one `server_url` validation event and one breadcrumb; tapping enabled Sign In retained the URL error and displayed missing username/password errors without duplicate telemetry, `login_attempted`/`login_failed`, request, spinner, fatal or unexpected exception. No credential values entered. Login tests 8/8 and analytics sanitizer tests 16/16 passed with zero failures; `:androidApp:assembleDebug` passed and installed/pulled APK hashes match. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; only local DebugAnalyticsManager output is evidenced. Crashlytics is N-A for ordinary validation and delivery remains unverified. See [original corrected case-17 run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-corrected-retest.txt) and [regression run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-empty-submit-regression.txt).
- **Discovery/failure/fix commits:** Original pre-fix gap and evidence `33a702168e5845bb126ee40e2c399b353d4de7d7`; first candidate duplicate failure/evidence `84d7f9954b3f994727d0703d9a7bb034b878a453`; focused verified fix `edebeb69c0f254888870b7209ad124b1ab5fd0c1`; QA-BUG-0037 validation UI/telemetry regression fix `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc`. Regression evidence and commit bookkeeping are recorded in the following documentation commit.

## QA-BUG-0037 — Empty Login submit cannot show required-field validation

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; before the fix, tapping Sign In on an empty form gave no field-specific explanation and the disabled control prevented submit-time validation.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the Samsung; button-submit, invalid-URL regression and IME variants pass.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Login credentials form; cases 18 and 757; case-17 adjacent regression.
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; pre-fix package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `edebeb69c0f254888870b7209ad124b1ab5fd0c1`, APK SHA-256 `ad132c11b257d5ae8294d932319e38643b941ef772df0b9988297ce2024d2e85`, PID `9237`; fix source `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc`, APK SHA-256 `99804cc1ef26d7f1deeb65de3ff359c90481c8034f99c7edadb08c8396e58450`, PID `22587`. Local and installed APK hashes match; package remains 0.4.5 (21).
- **Preconditions:** Settings → Servers → Add Server; Storyteller selected; URL at `https://` placeholder; username/password empty.
- **Exact reproduction:** With all fields empty, tap Sign In. Observe enabled/disabled state, field errors, loading/navigation, and PID-scoped Analytics/diagnostic/fatal logs.
- **Expected:** Case 18 requires field-level required errors and no network submission. Emit one bounded validation outcome and diagnostic breadcrumb for the empty-submit attempt, without a login attempt/failure or Crashlytics non-fatal. No raw field values.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix Sign In was disabled, so no errors or validation telemetry could be shown. The fixed form permits a submit attempt while not loading, then displays `Server URL is required`, `Username is required` and `Password is required`. It also handles the Samsung keyboard action through the same validation path. No request, auth attempt, spinner, navigation or crash.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix 1/1 empty-form tap had no errors/events/breadcrumbs. Fixed build: 1/1 button submit and 1/1 IME submit each showed all required errors and emitted exactly one bounded validation event plus one breadcrumb; 1/1 invalid-URL submit regression retained the URL error, showed missing credential errors, and added no duplicate event. PID `22587` remained alive.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-018-empty-submit-prefx.txt), [pre-fix blank form](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-018-empty-login-before-submit.png), [pre-fix after tap](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-018-empty-login-after-submit.png), [corrected case-18 run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-018-empty-submit-corrected-retest.txt) and [corrected errors](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-018-empty-login-corrected-errors.png), [case-17 regression run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-017-invalid-url-empty-submit-regression.txt), and [case-757 IME run](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-757-empty-login-ime-retest.txt) with [empty form](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-757-empty-login-ime-before.png), [focused field](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-757-empty-login-ime-focused.png), [after IME](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-757-empty-login-ime-after.png) and [keyboard hidden](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-757-empty-login-ime-errors-no-keyboard.png).
- **Root cause:** Required-field emptiness was represented only as disabled-button gating rather than validation state. No username/password error state or submit-attempt trigger existed; the disabled control made ViewModel validation unreachable.
- **Affected files:** `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/login/LoginViewState.kt`, `LoginScreen.kt`, `LoginViewModel.kt`, `LoginValidationTelemetry.kt`, Login host tests, `lib/analytics/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/analytics/implementation/AnalyticsParameterSanitizer.kt` and its tests, plus required-field strings in `translations/src/commonMain/composeResources/values/strings.xml`.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc` (`fix(login): show required field errors [QA-BUG-0037, case 18]`). The Sign In button and password IME dispatch validation whenever not loading; ViewModel tracks that validation was attempted, populates per-field required errors, blocks before the login gate/use case, and emits one bounded `required_fields_missing` outcome/breadcrumb. No credentials are included; ordinary validation does not create Crashlytics issues.
- **Retest:** PASS on committed source `24973eb0`, APK SHA-256 `99804cc1ef26d7f1deeb65de3ff359c90481c8034f99c7edadb08c8396e58450`, PID `22587`, with local and Samsung APK hashes identical. Button submit and case-757 IME action each rendered all three field-level errors, emitted exactly one safe local `login_validation_failed` event and one correlated `login_validation` breadcrumb, and produced no auth attempt/request/loading/navigation/crash. Case 17 invalid-URL regression retained its inline URL error, added missing credential messages and did not duplicate telemetry. Host Login tests 8/8 and sanitizer tests 16/16 passed; Android debug assembly passed. Firebase ingestion is waived; local logs only are evidenced. Crashlytics N-A for expected validation; delivery remains separately unverified. See corrected case-18, case-17 regression and case-757 evidence above.
- **Discovery/fix commits:** Discovery and pre-fix screenshots/report `8b2b30808324e3ffc01de45be0af3fd001714463`; focused fix `24973eb08704eafac6920b969e2c41a0aed3bffc`. Corrected-build results, evidence and documentation commit bookkeeping are in the next QA documentation commit.

## QA-BUG-0038 — Add Server tap is reported as a completed server addition

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; no server/account data was lost, but feature reporting falsely counts an uncompleted Add Server navigation as a successful registration and records `server_type=unknown`.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED on the committed Samsung build for case 443 Add Server navigation. Preserve the pre-fix false-success observation below.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management → Add Server → Login; case 443 (observed while setting up Login case 19).
- **Device/build/commit:** Physical Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source commit `e4d364256932ccdee158e3d1d08917eb5f9ba267`, APK SHA-256 `db800e023801ada7c7fd31347318f58c747ded9b002c0246ea729f3217615703`, PID `6531`. Local and Samsung-pulled APK hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** Settings → Servers → Server Management shows `No servers configured`; no server/account exists. DebugAnalyticsManager local provider is active.
- **Exact reproduction:** At 07:16:10 CEST on 2026-09-27, tap Add Server once; observe Login opens; back out without entering credentials or submitting; verify the server list remains empty. Inspect PID-scoped Analytics and diagnostic output.
- **Expected:** Count this tap as an Add Server/navigation attempt, not as a completed server addition. A success-named `server_added` event must wait until registration/persistence actually succeeds; no guessed server type or user data should be sent. Login route exposure is recorded only after the destination is visible. This normal navigation needs no Crashlytics non-fatal.
- **Actual:** One `server_added` event with `server_type=unknown` is emitted before `onNavigateToLogin()`. About 184 ms later the destination emits `login_screen_viewed` (`entry_point=add_server`) and a visible-route diagnostic breadcrumb. After leaving Login, the list still says `No servers configured`; no authentication or persistence occurred.
- **Frequency:** Confirmed 1/1 on-device Add Server tap; source audit confirms the event is synchronously emitted on every `OnAddServerClick` before navigation.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix run, timestamps, logs and source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-443-add-server-telemetry-prefx.txt); [pre-fix empty Server Management](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-0038-empty-server-management-after-add-tap.png); [committed-build retest and logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-443-add-server-telemetry-fixed-retest.txt), [visible Login](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-443-add-server-attempt-fixed-login-visible.png) and [unchanged empty list](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-443-add-server-attempt-fixed-empty-list.png). No credentials were entered; no account/server was created.
- **Root cause:** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt` maps the Add Server intent to `ServerManagementAnalyticsEvent.ServerAdded(serverType = "unknown")`, then navigates. The event records a click as a completed outcome and fires before authentication/registry persistence.
- **Affected files:** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt`; typed event definitions/tests in `lib/analytics/api/.../BookAnalyticsEvent.kt` and `lib/analytics/implementation/.../AnalyticsParameterSanitizerTest.kt`; case-443 evidence/results/run report.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c` (`fix(analytics): report Add Server as attempt [QA-BUG-0038, case 443]`). The Add Server tap now emits typed `server_add_attempted` with bounded source/destination, action, stage and started outcome; it omits server type rather than guessing `unknown`. The actual Login route exposure continues to emit only when visible.
- **Retest:** PASS on committed source `107d017c`, package 0.4.5 (21), Samsung PID `19860`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`; local and Samsung-pulled hashes match. One tap produced exactly one `server_add_attempted`, then one `login_screen_viewed` and visible-route breadcrumb; no `server_added`, `unknown` type, duplicate, credential, auth attempt or failure.
