Back returned to `No servers configured`. Analytics sanitizer tests 18/18 passed and debug assembly succeeded. Firebase ingestion is waived; local logs only are evidenced. Crashlytics N-A for expected navigation; delivery remains separately unverified. See fixed retest evidence above. Successful credential persistence is separately measured by existing `login_succeeded`; a credentials-success journey remains case 22 and has not been retested here.
- **Discovery/fix/documentation commits:** Pre-fix bug record and evidence committed in `12370c0b597747653e5736250b536c59c6faad64`; focused source fix `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`. Corrected-build result/evidence and commit bookkeeping are in the next QA documentation commit. Preserve the pre-fix observation after correction.

## QA-BUG-0039 — Server Management has no actual screen-exposure event

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; reporting cannot identify visits to Server Management or the source/entry path, so use and abandonment around server-account management are invisible.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG; implementation commit `dad9df1abeb1ef62a73bc29b71dc7dad559c01b0`.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management entry/back and journey reconstruction; cases 428, 440–441, 443, 747, 749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Discovery audit used Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`. Candidate verification build/PID/hash are in the retest entry below.
- **Preconditions:** Navigate from Settings/App Settings into `HomeDestination.ServerManagement`.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit of `HomeNavigation.kt`, `ServerManagementScreen.kt`, `ServerManagementViewModel.kt` and `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`; no Server Management destination-visible callback/event exists. The current PID's case-443 logs include an Add Server attempt and subsequent Login exposure, but no Server Management exposure event was implemented.
- **Expected:** Emit one bounded screen-view event and matching visible breadcrumb only when Server Management actually becomes the visible destination; include stable source/entry attribution and avoid recomposition/tab duplicates. A button tap alone is not proof of exposure.
- **Actual:** Server Management renders but has no exposure event or route breadcrumb. Its ViewModel logs only Add attempt, logout and remove events.
- **Frequency:** Every visit is unmeasured by source; runtime count not yet sampled in a focused pre-fix visit.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); source locations listed above. Case-443 screenshots/logs retain the Add Server journey but do not claim a screen exposure.
- **Root cause:** Screen exposure instrumentation was added for Welcome, Login and Home but not for nested Home destinations such as Server Management.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigation.kt`, `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`; typed navigation/server-management events and sanitizer tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `dad9df1abeb1ef62a73bc29b71dc7dad559c01b0` (`fix(analytics): report Server Management exposure [QA-BUG-0039, case 440]`). Adds one actual-visible Server Management exposure event and matching breadcrumb with bounded source/entry attribution, guarded by destination changes rather than recomposition.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung candidate APK SHA-256 `d86d8948dba8feb3cc28bb7e6e56b972513889b0d27a8d1a64c2533cf6e7f9ab`, PID `18779`. Opened Settings → Servers, returned by toolbar Back, then reopened. Exactly one `server_management_screen_viewed` and one matching visible-route breadcrumb appeared per actual visit (2 each total); repeated accessibility inspection produced no duplicates. Bounded context was `source_screen=app_settings`, `entry_point=servers_row`; no credentials or URL in event/log. Analytics evidence is local DebugAnalyticsManager only under the user waiver, not Firebase delivery. No failure was induced; Crashlytics is N-A for these successful route exposures and delivery remains unverified. [Candidate device evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-visible-route-and-card-prefx.txt).
- **Discovery / fix commits:** `41380c912945955438e18eb22568815de55997db` records the pre-fix source finding; `dad9df1abeb1ef62a73bc29b71dc7dad559c01b0` contains the fix. Preserve the source-level finding and initial absence after retest.

## QA-BUG-0040 — Logout and remove are reported as successes before registry completion

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; a failed logout/removal can be counted as successful even while the server/credentials remain or state becomes inconsistent. There are no distinct accepted-attempt and failure outcomes.
- **Status:** IMPLEMENTED and host-regression-tested in `6fe70f4c74eac14a95962aad0ab70d9fc941fcca`; Samsung case 445 now verifies the simple logout-success path. Remove case 447, active-activity case 446 and device failure/cancellation behavior remain untested, so keep this combined bug partially verified/open. Registry-write runtime failure remains separately tracked in QA-BUG-0043.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management logout/remove; cases 445–447, 726, 728–729; shared analytics integrity cases 748–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Source audit used Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`, source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`. Successful-logout pre-fix runtime: same Samsung, package 0.4.5 (21), source `9a782d9220f6f24b67fcde80143a8f721ed430b1`, PID `12486`, APK SHA-256 `6945eaeef0857d68527beaf5084b557ade2e832e6a54b5c64337039f5d18ca8d`.
- **Preconditions:** A card action invokes `OnLogoutClick` or `OnRemoveClick`; the backing registry mutation may complete or throw.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit: `onLogoutClick` logs `ServerLoggedOut` before `clearCredentials`; `onRemoveClick` logs `ServerRemoved` before `removeServer`. Both await registry work only afterward. No operation-attempt event, failure event, diagnostic breadcrumb or local error/retry state exists.
- **Expected:** Record a bounded attempt at the accepted action; emit the existing success-named event only after state mutation/persistence completes. On unexpected failure, leave an honest recoverable UI state, emit one bounded failure outcome and operation breadcrumbs, and report one actionable non-fatal only if user experience is materially broken. Ordinary cancellation must not be reported as success or exception.
- **Actual (pre-fix):** A failure after the premature event left a success-named event in logs; thrown registry errors were not handled in the ViewModel. The fixed candidate moves success emission after the registry operation returns.
- **Frequency (pre-fix):** Every logout/remove activation emitted its success-named event before the corresponding suspend operation returned. Logout was reproduced once on the Samsung; the fixed simple logout path is separately verified below. Remove/failure runtime variants remain untested.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); [case-445 Samsung pre-fix observation](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-445-logout-telemetry-prefx.txt); [case-445 QA-BUG-0040 fixed retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-445-logout-telemetry-fixed-retest.txt); `ServerManagementViewModel.kt` and `ServerManagementOperation.kt`. The pre-fix device emitted only `server_logged_out` with no operation breadcrumbs; the fixed candidate emitted the expected attempt/success pair.
- **Root cause:** Completion events are emitted at intent acceptance rather than at operation terminal boundaries; exceptions are not converted to recoverable operation state.
- **Affected files:** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt`, `ServerManagementScreen.kt`, `ServerManagementViewState.kt`, typed events/sanitizer/tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `6fe70f4c74eac14a95962aad0ab70d9fc941fcca` (`fix(settings): report server mutation outcomes [QA-BUG-0040, case 445]`). Adds typed logout/remove attempt, failure and cancellation events; retains the existing terminal success names but moves them after the registry returns; correlates operation breadcrumbs and reports one exception at the UI boundary. A bounded retry snackbar is shown for caught failures, with single-flight action gating. Host tests cover operation ordering, both success operation types, failure, retry dimension, cancellation/rethrow and one contextual exception.
- **Retest:** Host suite **PASS**: Server Management operation 3/3 and Analytics sanitizer 22/22. On Samsung source `7ffeca90`, case 445 simple logout **PASS**: one `server_logout_attempted` then `server_logged_out`, one correlated started/succeeded breadcrumb pair, and the single card changed from logged-in to `Not logged in` with Login available. Local debug Analytics only; Firebase ingestion waived. No crash; no failure was induced. [Case-445 fixed retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-445-logout-telemetry-fixed-retest.txt). Active-playback case 446, Remove case 447, and safe registry-write failure/cancellation retests remain NOT RUN; do not claim full QA-BUG-0040 closure.
- **Discovery / observation commits:** `41380c912945955438e18eb22568815de55997db` contains the pre-fix source audit; `1582fec7a35a69bf898cec6b5f74d2f972020090` records the Samsung case-445 success-path finding before the implementation commit. Keep both observations.

## QA-BUG-0041 — Logged-out, expired and failed server cards have no Login/Retry action

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; users cannot reconnect an existing server from its NotAuthenticated/TokenExpired/AuthenticationFailed card even though the catalogue expects recovery.
- **Status:** CONFIRMED on Samsung before fix; fix **VERIFIED for `NotAuthenticated`** on Samsung in `7ffeca90fffdb763009e19a8983691d204dde814`. TokenExpired/AuthenticationFailed variants remain untested, so keep the broader bug open until those variants are verified.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 444, 448–449; recovery 729.
- **Device/build/commit:** Pre-fix source audit on Samsung build `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, package 0.4.5 (21), APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`. Samsung pre-fix confirmation used source `6fe70f4c74eac14a95962aad0ab70d9fc941fcca`, PID `28336`, APK SHA-256 `98f42fdfedad993d2ae49f3149f0d12e0cf65008e818d06349e6e51fdb5e4e7f`. Candidate partial retest: PID `1746`, package 0.4.5 (21), APK SHA-256 `ccdc973f191804446bea71fe49f65a1d73b7f96ae2d0339385a57c11ff4839bf`; local/Samsung hashes match. Candidate source changes are uncommitted, based on `876c41af696baab364cc56138335c4d130b62547`.
- **Preconditions:** A registered server card is NotAuthenticated, TokenExpired or AuthenticationFailed.
- **Exact reproduction:** After case 445 left the only remote server logged out, opened Settings → Servers on the QA-BUG-0040 candidate and inspected the card via accessibility hierarchy. It showed `Not logged in` and a server type, but no Login or Retry control; Remove was present. Source audit of `ServerManagementScreen.kt` confirms no dispatch of `OnLoginClick` for any card.
- **Expected:** Each recoverable unauthenticated/expired/failed state offers an accessible Login/Retry action targeting that server and preserves truthful state until reauthentication completes.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix, no such action was rendered or dispatched. The candidate now renders an accessible `Log in` or `Retry` button for supported remote server types and routes to the selected existing server's Login form. The Samsung `NotAuthenticated` variant successfully reauthenticated and returned to the connected card; other supported auth states remain unverified.
- **Frequency:** Source composition omits recovery for all non-authenticated card states; confirmed on-device once for `NotAuthenticated`. Expired/failed states remain untested.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); [case-444 Samsung pre-fix observation](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-444-logged-out-card-login-action-prefx.txt); [candidate route/prefill partial retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-444-existing-server-login-route-candidate.txt); `ServerManagementScreen.kt` card branches.
- **Root cause:** Card actions implement only Logout-for-authenticated and Remove; the declared login intent was not wired to UI.
- **Affected files:** `ServerManagementScreen.kt`, `ServerManagementViewModel.kt`, translations/accessibility labels and tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `7ffeca90fffdb763009e19a8983691d204dde814` (`fix(settings): reauthenticate existing server from card [QA-BUG-0041, case 444]`). Adds an accessible Login/Retry control, carries only the existing server ID through navigation, loads the registered server's saved type/URL from the repository, routes back to the preserved Server Management card after success, and persists credentials under the existing ID rather than adding a duplicate. Adds bounded card-login attempt analytics and actual Login-route attribution; username, password, URL and server ID are not analytics parameters.
- **Retest:** Pre-fix Samsung case 444 **FAIL** for `NotAuthenticated`: no Login/Retry control. Candidate **PASS** for the logged-out card action, existing-server form, locked saved configuration, successful authentication and return to the original single connected card (historical QA fixture retest); one rejected attempt is separately preserved. On the final combined committed build, the read-only route was rechecked: one `server_login_attempted`, one visible Login exposure attributed `server_management/server_card_login`, one correlated config-load started/prefilled pair, then toolbar Back returned to the unchanged logged-out card. Credentials were not submitted and no Retry used to preserve the retained fixture. Local debug Analytics only; Firebase ingestion waived. [Final-build route-only evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-29/case-444-logged-out-card-login-route-final-build.txt). TokenExpired/AuthenticationFailed fixtures remain untested; keep the overall bug open for those variants.
- **Discovery / confirmation / fix commits:** `41380c912945955438e18eb22568815de55997db` records the source audit; `876c41af696baab364cc56138335c4d130b62547` records Samsung's pre-fix card failure; `446bf543489acf7dce5ef942388231dfefba8c58` records the route/prefill partial retest; `7ffeca90fffdb763009e19a8983691d204dde814` contains the focused implementation, regression coverage and successful `NotAuthenticated` retest. Preserve all historical observations. TokenExpired/AuthenticationFailed remain outstanding.

## QA-BUG-0042 — Remove Server deletes immediately without confirmation

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; an accidental tap can remove a server connection and associated credentials without the confirmation/cancel protection specified by the test plan.
- **Status:** CONFIRMED by source audit; Samsung interaction not yet exercised.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 447 and 726; removal source isolation case 728.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`.
- **Preconditions:** A Server Management card is rendered.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit: tapping the card's Delete `IconButton` immediately dispatches `OnRemoveClick`; ViewModel immediately launches `serverRegistry.removeServer`. No dialog or pending-confirmation state exists.
- **Expected:** Open a clearly identified destructive confirmation; Cancel/Back leaves server, credentials and books unchanged; only explicit confirmation starts removal.
- **Actual:** The first tap starts removal immediately, so cases 726 cancellation and case-447 confirmation cannot be performed as specified.
- **Frequency:** Every Delete icon activation.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); `ServerManagementScreen.kt` lines 153–155 and 216–223.
- **Root cause:** Remove is wired directly to the operation intent with no confirmation UI.
- **Affected files:** `ServerManagementScreen.kt`, `ServerManagementViewModel.kt`, `ServerManagementViewState.kt`, translations and tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Pending. Add a confirmation with accessible server-type/name context that avoids sensitive URLs/usernames; dispatch removal only after confirmation and record cancellation separately.
- **Retest:** NOT RUN. Verify dialog content, Cancel, outside/system Back where supported, explicit confirm, and no duplicate removal on rapid taps. Keep other server/local data intact.
- **Discovery commit:** Pending documentation commit; preserve pre-fix source finding.

## QA-BUG-0043 — Server registry logout/removal persistence failures can leave stale in-memory state

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; a local preference write failure can leave UI state mutated while persisted server/credential data remains stale, causing apparent logout/removal to reverse after restart or orphaned credentials.
- **Status:** CONFIRMED in source mutation ordering; failure-path device behavior untested and may be blocked by missing safe write-fault fixture.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 445, 447, 729; shared persistence/recovery cases 741–743, 748–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`.
- **Preconditions:** `ServerRegistry.clearCredentials` or `removeServer` mutates StateFlows and a subsequent preferences write throws.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit: `clearCredentials` removes the credential from `_credentials` before `persistCredentials`; `removeServer` removes server and credentials from `_servers`/`_credentials` before `persistServers` and `persistCredentials`. Neither method uses the existing `persistStateMutation` rollback helper for these transitions.
- **Expected:** On a failed local commit, restore coherent observable/persisted state or report a clearly recoverable partial outcome; never claim success or present a state that silently reverses after restart.
- **Actual:** A thrown preference write can escape after in-memory state changed; exact persisted/in-memory state under a mid-operation write fault has not yet been executed.
- **Frequency:** Conditional on logout/removal persistence failure.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); `ServerRegistryImpl.kt` lines 172–181 and 282–292; existing rollback helper is used for add/save paths but not these operations.
- **Root cause:** Multi-map mutation and preference persistence are not guarded by rollback/compensation for logout/remove.
- **Affected files:** `lib/server/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/server/implementation/ServerRegistryImpl.kt`, registry common tests, Server Management recovery UI/diagnostics.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Pending. Add transaction/rollback behavior and targeted fault-injection unit tests; preserve cancellation and classify rollback failure distinctly.
- **Retest:** Host fault-injection test NOT RUN; Samsung controlled write-failure variant BLOCKED until a safe app-scoped fixture is available. Normal logout/remove is not a substitute. Preserve the logged-in demo server until planned verification is complete.
- **Discovery commit:** Pending documentation commit; preserve source-level finding and its unverified runtime scope.

## QA-BUG-0044 — Server list observation failure has no terminal error or recovery state

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; if the combined registry/auth-state flow fails, the Server Management screen can remain on a spinner indefinitely or lose its observation coroutine with no retry guidance.
- **Status:** FIXED IN SOURCE; injected host failure/retry/cancellation coverage PASS. Samsung route validation is pending the committed candidate; safe device failure injection is BLOCKED.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 440, 451, 484, 729, 742, 748, 777.
- **Pre-fix device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`.
- **Preconditions:** Either upstream registry/auth-state flow throws before a successful emission.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit: `observeServers()` combines both flows and uses `onEach { isLoading=false }` then `launchIn(viewModelScope)` with no `catch`; initial view state is `isLoading=true`, and has no error/retry field. `ServerManagementScreen` therefore has only spinner, empty, and card branches.
- **Expected:** End loading on an upstream failure, distinguish failure from an empty list, show a usable retry/recovery action, log one bounded failure outcome/breadcrumb and report only unexpected user-impacting failures once.
- **Actual:** No failure state or recovery branch exists. Runtime impact and whether the coroutine exception handler terminates the app have not been observed.
- **Frequency:** Conditional on an upstream flow failure.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); `ServerManagementViewModel.kt` lines 43–65; `ServerManagementViewState.kt` fields 5–8; `ServerManagementScreen.kt` lines 117–160.
- **Root cause:** Observation pipeline handles only successful emissions and conflates the initial loading state with no terminal failure state.
- **Affected files:** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/{ServerManagementViewModel.kt,ServerManagementViewState.kt,ServerManagementIntent.kt,ServerManagementScreen.kt,ServerListObservation.kt}`; server-list Analytics API and sanitizer tests; translations; `ServerListObservationTest.kt`.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Focused fix commit pending. Adds terminal load success/failure/cancellation outcomes, source-observation failure context, a recoverable Retry UI, preserves previously loaded cards if a later observation fails, and reports one non-fatal at the UI boundary. Cancellation is rethrown without a non-fatal.
- **Retest:** Host PASS: Server Management UI suite 13/13, Analytics sanitizer suite 50/50; Android debug assembly and iOS Simulator source compilation pass. Injected tests cover initial source failure and no-value completion, retry success, post-load stream failure, correlated breadcrumbs, single exception reporting, and cancellation-without-report. [Host evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-29/qa-bug-0044-server-list-recovery-host-tests.txt). Samsung normal route/load revalidation remains NOT RUN pending the committed candidate build. Safe Samsung failure injection is BLOCKED; do not claim device recovery or Crashlytics delivery. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived.
- **Discovery commit:** Pending documentation commit; preserve source-level finding.

## QA-BUG-0045 — Successful Add Server has no server-added completion event

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; after QA-BUG-0038 was corrected to an attempt event, metrics record Add Server navigation and `login_succeeded` separately but never emit the typed `server_added` completion, so abandoned and successfully registered additions cannot be counted as one operation.
- **Status:** CONFIRMED by source audit; current case-21 successful server persistence did not emit a `server_added` event.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 22, 33, 443–444, 727, 749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), source `107d017cdcc3d834e99dc983e20a7ed6002e319c`, APK SHA-256 `64b83815f96371c00407dbf5955968064348ba4aadaa0497edc659bfe1891304`, PID `19860`.
- **Preconditions:** Tap Add Server and complete authentication plus local server/credential persistence.
- **Exact reproduction:** Source audit found `ServerAdded` only defined in the event API and no call site after commit `107d017c`; case-21 PID logs show `server_add_attempted` on the pre-auth navigation in case 443 and then Login success, but no server-added completion event.
- **Expected:** Emit `server_added` with bounded actual server type only after a new server registration and credentials persist; do not emit it for failed/abandoned login or ordinary reauthentication. Existing `login_succeeded` remains the authentication outcome.
- **Actual:** No server-add completion event is emitted at persistence success.
- **Frequency:** Each successful newly-added server under the current source.
- **Evidence:** [Source-audit/action map](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-source-audit-prefx.txt); `ServerManagementAnalyticsEvent.ServerAdded` is defined but unused; Login's success event is distinct.
- **Root cause:** QA-BUG-0038's false early success event was replaced by an Add Server attempt, but no successful registration boundary was instrumented afterward.
- **Affected files:** Login persistence/success boundary and entry-point context, analytics API/sanitizer/tests, Server Management results.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Pending. Correlate Add Server entry to persisted new-server success (not simply every Login success); emit one bounded completion only after persistence and no credentials/URL/username.
- **Retest:** NOT RUN. Use one successful Add Server flow and one abandoned/failed flow; require one attempt, completion only for the persisted new server, and no duplicate on recomposition. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; local DebugAnalyticsManager evidence only.
- **Discovery commit:** Pending documentation commit; preserve source-level gap.

## QA-BUG-0046 — Server Management card does not show the server type

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; users cannot distinguish a card's server implementation/type as required, especially when server names are identical or ambiguous.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG for the authenticated Storyteller variant; Audiobookshelf variant remains BLOCKED for lack of a second-server fixture. Implementation commit `a092dbaa4de6c986e14d0f454310b4a9d3cb00fc`.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Cases 22, 442; server-card inspection and accessibility variants.
- **Device/build/commit:** Pre-fix discovery used Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), PID `18779`, APK SHA-256 `d86d8948dba8feb3cc28bb7e6e56b972513889b0d27a8d1a64c2533cf6e7f9ab`. Fix verification used PID `5881`, APK SHA-256 `6e2a5b64d004d81debfa484305acc86c8c254ad29f693c3c73cc6f63e63901dd`, exact source tree later committed as `a092dbaa4de6c986e14d0f454310b4a9d3cb00fc`; local and pulled APK hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** At least one server card is visible in Server Management.
- **Exact reproduction:** Open Settings → Servers and inspect the authenticated Storyteller card. The safe UI-tree audit confirmed the card and authenticated status without retaining the username or URL. Source inspection of `ServerManagementScreen.ServerListItem` shows `server.name`, `server.baseUrl`, and `authState.toDisplayString()` only; `server.type` is not rendered.
- **Expected:** Each card displays a distinct, accessible server type (e.g. Storyteller or Audiobookshelf) as well as server name and connection state, without relying on the server name to imply its type.
- **Actual:** The card displays server name, base URL and auth state, but no separate server type. Case 442 fails its required server-name/type/connection-state inspection.
- **Frequency:** Every card, independent of auth state.
- **Evidence:** [Candidate device check and safe log excerpt](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-visible-route-and-card-prefx.txt); source `ServerManagementScreen.kt`, `ServerListItem` and `toDisplayString` (lines 164–237). No raw UI tree, account identifier, URL, screenshot or credential was retained.
- **Root cause:** The card composable never reads/renders `ServerUiModel.type`.
- **Affected files:** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementScreen.kt`, translations, and focused UI tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `a092dbaa4de6c986e14d0f454310b4a9d3cb00fc` (`fix(settings): show server type on cards [QA-BUG-0046, case 442]`). Adds the localized resource `settings_server_type_label` and renders `server.type.displayName` as visible text; it does not infer type from the user-authored name or expose a URL.
- **Retest:** Storyteller-authenticated card **PASS** on the hash-matched Samsung build; the exact source tree was committed after the run with no source changes. UI hierarchy confirmed `Server type: Storyteller`, authenticated-state text, and accessible Logout/Remove actions; route event/breadcrumb remained exactly once on the single actual exposure. Analytics N-A for the static label itself; no failure was induced, so Crashlytics is N-A. No username/URL, screenshot or raw hierarchy retained. Audiobookshelf card is **BLOCKED** because no second-server account/fixture is available; do not treat enum-backed rendering as execution evidence. Large-font/TalkBack variants remain unrun. [Case-442 retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-442-server-type-card-retest.txt).
- **Discovery / fix commits:** `909ba9c492936c06a914332b5439f3d820c80d84` records the original failing card observation; `a092dbaa4de6c986e14d0f454310b4a9d3cb00fc` contains the fix. Preserve the pre-fix observation.

## QA-BUG-0047 — Nested Server Management Back has no navigation outcome telemetry

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; Server Management exits are invisible to journey analytics and diagnostics, obscuring where users return or abandon the route.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG; verified source commit `de8cfec65df74d569b34ae3d56a7495cf62c9800`.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Case 441 toolbar Back; shared Back/navigation variants 740, 747–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Pre-fix discovery used Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), PID `18779`, APK SHA-256 `d86d8948dba8feb3cc28bb7e6e56b972513889b0d27a8d1a64c2533cf6e7f9ab`. The fixed Samsung build is PID `28758`, APK SHA-256 `b122bd7fe9dfe927885ca96f4f82838aea8e864543d31d4ecc1030944bb41ac0`, source commit `de8cfec65df74d569b34ae3d56a7495cf62c9800`; local and pulled hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** Server Management is the visible destination, opened from App Settings.
- **Exact reproduction:** Tap toolbar Back (content description `Back`). App Settings becomes visible, but the PID-scoped 09:46 log contains no `navigation_back`/destination event and no diagnostic breadcrumb for the completed Server Management → App Settings transition. The matching screen-view event only records entry.
- **Expected:** On an accepted Back that actually changes the visible route, record one bounded navigation outcome with source `server_management`, destination `app_settings`, and entry point `toolbar_back`, plus a matching breadcrumb. Distinguish attempted from completed navigation where appropriate; do not emit a completion on a no-op or report ordinary Back to Crashlytics.
- **Actual:** Back is functionally applied, but no nested route-exit Analytics event or diagnostic breadcrumb is emitted. Existing `HomeNavigationViewModel` handles `GoBack` by emitting only `HomeNavigationEvent.GoBack`.
- **Frequency:** Every Server Management toolbar Back under current instrumentation; system Back and swipe remain untested.
- **Evidence:** [Candidate device check and safe log excerpt](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-visible-route-and-card-prefx.txt); `HomeNavigationViewModel.onIntent(GoBack)` and `HomeNavigation.kt` Back application path. PID logs show only the two Server Management entry pairs and no Back outcome.
- **Root cause:** Nested Home `GoBack` navigation has no analytics/diagnostic terminal callback; only the Welcome root has a typed `navigation_back` event.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`, `HomeNavigation.kt`, typed navigation Analytics API/sanitizer/tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `de8cfec65df74d569b34ae3d56a7495cf62c9800` (`fix(analytics): report applied nested Back outcomes [QA-BUG-0047, case 441]`). Nested Home Back now carries bounded source/destination/entry context, emits `navigation_back_attempted` and a correlated start breadcrumb, then records `navigation_back` and a terminal breadcrumb only after the navigation owner applies the pop. Failed application uses one contextual non-fatal.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung candidate APK SHA-256 `b122bd7fe9dfe927885ca96f4f82838aea8e864543d31d4ecc1030944bb41ac0`, PID `28758`. Both toolbar Back and Android system Back returned to App Settings; each emitted exactly one bounded attempt and one successful completion with matching start/terminal breadcrumbs and route-specific entry point. Repeated UI-tree inspection did not duplicate events. No private values were logged; no failure was induced, and ordinary Back produced no Crashlytics report. Local DebugAnalyticsManager only; Firebase ingestion is waived. [Full logs/results](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-back-retest-and-reader-back-prefx.txt).
- **Discovery / fix commits:** `909ba9c492936c06a914332b5439f3d820c80d84` records the pre-fix failure; `de8cfec65df74d569b34ae3d56a7495cf62c9800` contains the fix. Preserve the initial case-441 observation above and candidate device evidence.

## QA-BUG-0048 — Reader system Back is attributed as a close-button action

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; Analytics and diagnostics misclassify how the Reader was exited, weakening source-specific navigation and abandonment reporting.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG for Reader system Back and close button. Implementation commit `9a782d9220f6f24b67fcde80143a8f721ed430b1`.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Shared case 740/Reader/system-back; Reader exit/action mapping cases 138, 181, 290, 326, 740, 747–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Pre-fix Samsung run: `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package 0.4.5 (21), PID `28758`; APK SHA-256 `b122bd7fe9dfe927885ca96f4f82838aea8e864543d31d4ecc1030944bb41ac0`, source `de8cfec65df74d569b34ae3d56a7495cf62c9800`. Fixed retest: PID `12486`, APK SHA-256 `6945eaeef0857d68527beaf5084b557ade2e832e6a54b5c64337039f5d18ca8d`, exact source later committed as `9a782d9220f6f24b67fcde80143a8f721ed430b1`; local and Samsung-pulled hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** A Reader route is visible and the user performs Android system Back.
- **Exact reproduction:** Relaunch Parrot with its saved Reader route; after usable content appears, press Android system Back once. The Reader correctly returns to Books, but the generated `navigation_back_attempted`/`navigation_back` and diagnostic breadcrumbs use `entry_point=close_button`.
- **Expected:** System Back must use `entry_point=system_back`; the Reader close control must use its own stable entry point (e.g. `close_button`). Each actual route transition emits one attempt/completion and correlated breadcrumbs with accurate source, destination and entry point.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix, functional exit succeeded but the shared callback tagged system Back as `close_button`. The fixed callback now preserves the originating source.
- **Frequency:** Confirmed 1/1 on-device system Back; source path means every Reader system Back routed through `onClose` is misattributed.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix Samsung logs](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/server-management-back-retest-and-reader-back-prefx.txt) and [committed-fix Samsung retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/reader-back-attribution-fix-retest.txt). Before the fix, `ReaderScreen`'s `NavigationBackHandler` called `viewModel.close()` and Home assigned `close_button` to the shared callback.
- **Root cause:** Reader system Back and explicit close both converge on the same no-argument `onClose` callback, losing the originating navigation action before the Home-level telemetry boundary.
- **Affected files:** `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/ReaderScreen.kt`, `ReaderViewModel.kt`, Reader close intent/tests, and Home route callback/analytics context.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `9a782d9220f6f24b67fcde80143a8f721ed430b1` (`fix(reader): attribute system Back source [QA-BUG-0048, case 740]`). Carries a bounded `ReaderCloseSource` through the ReaderViewModel close callback; system Back uses `system_back`, the existing close control defaults to `close_button`. The existing asynchronous/non-cancellable progress/session checkpoint path is unchanged. Adds a regression test for the stable, bounded source values.
- **Retest:** Both variants **PASS** on the hash-matched Samsung build. System Back returned to Books, emitted exactly one attempted and one successful completion with `system_back` attribution, and produced correlated start/terminal breadcrumbs. Reopening the saved book restored page 29/64. The Close control separately returned to Books with exactly one attempt, one success, and matching breadcrumbs attributed to `close_button`. No duplicate or unexpected non-fatal/fatal markers. Local debug provider only; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived. Crashlytics is N-A because no failure was induced. [Retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/reader-back-attribution-fix-retest.txt).
- **Discovery / fix commits:** `ad717707ee355aaebbc7ce75dbf533727808b562` preserves the pre-fix Reader observation; `9a782d9220f6f24b67fcde80143a8f721ed430b1` contains the fix. Keep the pre-fix evidence above.

## QA-BUG-0049 — Logout leaves active ReadAloud playback running

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium; after the user logs out of a server, its audio continues to play in the background. This is confusing and may expose account-linked content after the user believes the session has ended.
- **Status:** CONFIRMED on Samsung in case 446; fix not yet implemented. Reproduced once with the single authenticated Storyteller card and active ReadAloud playback.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management cases 445–446; Reader ReadAloud playback; shared lifecycle/analytics cases 740, 747–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package 0.4.5 (21), PID `1746`; installed APK SHA-256 `ccdc973f191804446bea71fe49f65a1d73b7f96ae2d0339385a57c11ff4839bf`. App code corresponds to `7ffeca90fffdb763009e19a8983691d204dde814`; repository HEAD `5feb2b674460c719283826087ea2d6ec1fc3d9bb` was docs-only after the candidate build. QA-BUG-0040 logout instrumentation is from `6fe70f4c74eac14a95962aad0ab70d9fc941fcca`.
- **Preconditions:** An authenticated server has cached ReadAloud content and playback is active in Reader; one server card is present.
- **Exact reproduction:** Start ReadAloud playback, leave Reader with system Back while playback remains active, open Settings → Servers, tap the authenticated card's Logout once. The card changes to `Not logged in`; check the Android media session after logout.
- **Expected:** Logout must safely stop or finish the server's active playback, leave Server Management usable and avoid a crash. Emit bounded playback/lifecycle outcomes as appropriate; never log the book title/UUID, URL, credentials, or server ID.
- **Actual:** Logout returned successfully and the card became `Not logged in`, but Android media-session state remained `PLAYING` about 50 seconds later. There was no crash. Local logs contained one `playback_started`, one `server_logout_attempted` and one `server_logged_out`, plus a correlated logout start/success breadcrumb pair; no playback stop/pause outcome or diagnostic explained the continued audio.
- **Frequency:** Confirmed 1/1 on this Samsung candidate. Only one server was present; multi-server isolation remains untested.
- **Evidence:** [Sanitized case-446 pre-fix evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-446-logout-during-readaloud-playback-prefx.txt). No screenshot or book/account content retained.
- **Root cause:** Initial source trace indicates Server Management's logout mutation only clears registry credentials, while active narration is controlled through `NowPlayingProvider`; the logout flow does not request playback stop. Full lifecycle ownership and platform-specific behavior audit is pending.
- **Affected files (candidate; audit pending):** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt`, `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/playback/NowPlayingProvider.kt`, and `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigationViewModel.kt` / platform NowPlayingProvider implementation.
- **Fix reference / commit:** Pending. Expected to stop active server playback only after an accepted logout attempt and before reporting terminal success; preserve unrelated server playback semantics if applicable. Add focused operation regression coverage and bounded diagnostics/Analytics without sensitive identifiers.
- **Retest:** BLOCKED on the current candidate: only retained server is logged out and there is no authorized disposable authenticated playback fixture, so no new playback/logout action was attempted. The temporary `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` grant used for the historical failure run was restored to the original denied state on 2026-09-29; see [fixture blocker and cleanup evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-29/case-446-auth-fixture-blocker-and-permission-restore.txt). To close this bug, obtain a safe authenticated playback fixture, implement stop-on-logout with focused tests, then verify media session termination and telemetry on Samsung.

## QA-BUG-0050 — Expected logged-out last-book launch is reported as an exception

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; a normal logged-out server state created a non-actionable exception report and Crashlytics noise, obscuring unexpected Reader failures.
- **Status:** FIXED AND DIAGNOSTICALLY RETESTED ON SAMSUNG. Case 758 safe Retry/Back behavior is partially retested; authentication and saved-target reopen remain NOT RUN/BLOCKED due to the unavailable disposable login fixture.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Startup/last-book cases 11, 518–519, 758; Reader initialization/failure cases 138, 740, 747–749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; package 0.4.5 (21), PID `23017`; APK SHA-256 `1cf600c5f7ba9bc59d3c6ae5d48a1e1ddbe8cc773bdb528ba5e88bb96e326b8d`. Tested tree was based on `f519d7609d48b3619364244c2270abb8234e4f44` plus uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 candidate changes.
- **Preconditions:** A current book belongs to a server that has been logged out, and Open Last Book on Launch is enabled.
- **Exact reproduction:** After logout, replace/relaunch the installed Parrot APK in place with `adb install -r` and start MainActivity without clearing app data. Startup selects Home and tries to restore the last Reader route. The screen displays a not-authenticated error with Retry and a top navigation arrow. Logs show a last-book attempt and failed terminal outcome; the debug analytics provider also records one structured exception. The tested interruption was APK replacement/relaunch—not force-stop, Recents swipe, or ordinary process death.
- **Expected:** The launch should be classified as an expected authentication failure: retain one bounded failed last-book outcome and terminal diagnostic context, but do not report a Crashlytics exception for ordinary logged-out state. Retry must be separately counted; Back must recover to usable Books without clearing the saved target. Successful reauthentication/reopen must remain possible.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix startup produced `reader_initialization_failed` and one local exception report. After the fix, startup and one Retry each produced one attempt plus one `last_book_launch_completed(outcome=failed, reason_code=server_not_authenticated)`; system Back produced one attempt/success pair to Books. No exception report or fatal crash was observed. The recoverable error gives a generic message and Retry/Close, but no direct Login. The existing-server Login form is reachable through Settings → Servers, but both Username and Password were empty. Reauthentication/reopen remains untested pending an authorized disposable fixture.
- **Frequency:** Pre-fix exception confirmed 1/1. Fixed diagnostic behavior observed on Samsung for startup and one Retry; ordinary force-stop/process-death variants remain NOT RUN.
- **Evidence:** [Pre-fix evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-758-logged-out-last-book-launch-prefx.txt), [failed AuthError-only candidate](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-758-notfound-classification-failed-retest.txt), and [fixed-build retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-758-logged-out-last-book-launch-retest.txt). No screenshot, server/book identifier, URL, title, account, credential, raw throwable or raw UI hierarchy is retained.
- **Root cause (revised after candidate retest):** `GetBookByUuidUseCase` returned `AppError.NotFoundError("Server not found or not authenticated: $serverId")` when `AuthenticatedRepositoryProvider` had no repository for the server. Since `NotFoundError.shouldReportException` defaults to `true`, Reader reported the expected logged-out state as an exception; the dynamic message also contained the server identifier. An AuthError-only candidate did not fix the Samsung behavior because the actual error was `NotFoundError`.
- **Failed fix attempt:** An uncommitted candidate explicitly excluded `AuthError` and mapped that type to `server_not_authenticated`; build/tests passed but Samsung still produced the same `reader_initialization_failed` event and one exception because the actual error was `NotFoundError`. Preserve this failed attempt; it is not a fix or passing retest. [Evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-758-notfound-classification-failed-retest.txt).
- **Affected files:** `feature/books/domain/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/books/domain/usecase/GetBookByUuidUseCase.kt`, `base/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/base/result/AppResult.kt` and tests, and Reader last-book failure handling in `feature/reader/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/reader/ui/reader/ReaderViewModel.kt`.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `742167d891e044c7829a7ccb889f545d6e2f69b6` (`fix(reader): classify logged-out book restore [QA-BUG-0050, case 758]`). Missing authenticated repository now returns bounded `AuthError`; expected authentication failures are excluded from exception reporting; last-book Reader context uses `server_not_authenticated`. Added tests proving safe message/classification and preserving exception reporting for unexpected errors.
- **Retest:** Diagnostic/safe-navigation retest passed locally on Samsung: one startup failed outcome and one retry failed outcome with `server_not_authenticated`; Retry and Back were usable; Back returned to Books; zero exception reports and zero fatal crashes. Firebase Analytics/Crashlytics delivery is not claimed for this debug-provider check. Reauthentication and saved-target reopen remain NOT RUN/BLOCKED pending a disposable login fixture, so case 758 is not fully passed.

## QA-BUG-0051 — Rejected credentials do not surface the failed state on the server card

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; after an unsuccessful reauthentication attempt, the user is returned to a generic `Not logged in` card rather than seeing that the last attempt failed. The Log in action remains available, but the failure state and Retry affordance expected by the catalog are absent.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG for the rejected-login card/Retry path. Successful-login clearing is a separate NOT RUN variant because the configured endpoint rejected the test credentials; do not treat that branch as device-verified.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management cases 444 and 449; Login case 28/33 and case 758 reauthentication attempt.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 `S921BXXSGDZG1`; Parrot 0.4.5 (21), latest retest PID `21467`; APK SHA-256 `47b65964d36580f5e3decce808ccc89324c90b9fcd63092ccd980cae90ed8277`; QA-BUG-0051 fix commit `7b6178ee` (APK built from equivalent source plus separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 changes).
- **Preconditions:** Existing Storyteller server is present but logged out; Login form is opened from its card.
- **Exact reproduction:** Enter the authorized disposable test login only in Parrot; tap the actual Sign In button; after the server rejects the attempt, press system Back to return to Server Management. The card says `Not logged in` and offers `Log in`; it does not say `Login failed` or expose the expected Retry action.
- **Expected:** One credentials attempt and failed outcome are recorded. On returning to Server Management, the card reflects the failed state with an actionable retry/login path, without persisting credentials or producing a Crashlytics report for ordinary auth rejection.
- **Actual:** Local analytics recorded exactly one `login_attempted` then one `login_failed`; diagnostics recorded one `credentials_login` start/failure pair with `reason_code=auth_rejected`, and no exception/crash. The card reverted to `Not logged in` with `Log in`, so the user-visible failure state is missing.
- **Frequency:** Confirmed 1/1 on the tested Storyteller server. Other server types and transport-failure variants remain untested.
- **Evidence:** [Sanitized case-449 pre-fix evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-449-login-failure-card-prefx.txt). The device password-manager save prompt was declined. No screenshot, URL, username, credential, response body, or raw hierarchy is retained.
- **Root cause:** `ServerManagementViewModel.observeServers` renders `serverRegistry.observeAllAuthStates()`. After a failed Storyteller sign-in, no credential is stored, so `ServerRegistryImpl` falls back to `NotAuthenticated`; the login operation's failure is not propagated back to Server Management. Although `ServerAuthState.AuthenticationFailed` exists and has card rendering, source search found it is currently produced only by the Parrot Cloud provider, not the Storyteller Login flow.
- **Affected files (audit entry points):** `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt`, `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementScreen.kt`, Login completion/failure callback wiring, and `lib/server/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/server/implementation/ServerRegistryImpl.kt` / relevant server auth providers.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `7b6178ee` (`fix(settings): retain failed server login state [QA-BUG-0051, case 449]`). Root navigation retains a transient failed-server-ID marker from rejected existing-server Login, passes it to the underlying Server Management card, and clears it on the next accepted auth attempt or successful existing-server login. Cancellation does not set the marker; no credentials are persisted and expected auth rejection remains a non-exception outcome. Analytics remains the existing single `login_failed`; a correlated bounded `failure_state_retained` breadcrumb records propagation.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung for two candidate installs: a rejected Login displayed `Login failed` + `Retry`; Retry opened the existing-server Login form; system Back returned to the same failed card. The exact rebuild/install retest used PID `21467` and APK SHA-256 `47b65964d36580f5e3decce808ccc89324c90b9fcd63092ccd980cae90ed8277`; earlier candidate PID `13499`, SHA-256 `f25c5343b968763c16a9f9cd50b2dc6f7a5c32ac83b4b33eabe312a39b9e8709`. Local debug logs showed one attempt/failure and one matching failure-state breadcrumb, with zero exception reports/fatal crashes. Evidence: [case-449 failed-state retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-449-login-failure-card-retest.txt). The subsequent successful-login clearing branch remains NOT RUN because this configured endpoint rejected the test login. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics delivery is not established by local logs.

## QA-BUG-0052 — Server-card reauthentication retry is counted as a first login attempt

- **Severity / user impact:** Low; authentication retry rates and recovery funnels undercount retries after a user returns to Server Management and selects the failed server card's Retry action.
- **Status:** FIXED AND RETESTED ON SAMSUNG. The pre-fix `is_retry=false` defect is preserved below; after the fix, normal Log in is `false` while a failed-card Retry submit and its terminal failure are `true`.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Server Management case 449; Login case 23/525 and instrumentation case 534.
- **Device/build/commit:** Required Samsung `RFCWC0SSVDM`, model SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 (`S921BXXSGDZG1`). Pre-fix: Parrot 0.4.5 (21), PID `21467`, APK SHA-256 `47b65964d36580f5e3decce808ccc89324c90b9fcd63092ccd980cae90ed8277`, source `7b6178ee` plus QA-BUG-0049 candidate. Fixed retest: PID `1802`, APK SHA-256 `f3c4eb2c62bc21aec91c3c32f583227760d033b4591bd0ace804b15b8de58a65`, QA-BUG-0052 commit `2d4360996a620f4cba2d25c25bbd3e7cdaaeba88` plus separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 candidate; local and installed hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** An existing server card has a failed-login state; the user activates its Retry action and submits validly formed credentials in the new Login route.
- **Exact reproduction (Samsung):** From the visible failed Storyteller card, tap Retry, enter syntactically complete synthetic rejected credentials only in Parrot, dismiss the keyboard and tap Sign In. The form displayed its rejected-login error and remained usable. PID-scoped local debug event at 18:52:05.635 recorded `login_attempted` with `server_type=storyteller`, `auth_method=credentials`, `is_retry=false`, `outcome=started`, followed by `login_failed` at 18:52:05.740. The retry-start breadcrumb preceded it at 18:52:05.633. System Back opened Android's password-save prompt; selecting **No, thanks** returned to the failed card (`Login failed` + `Retry`). No synthetic input, URL, account name, screenshot or raw hierarchy was retained.
- **Expected:** The accepted authentication submit after a failed-card Retry emits one `login_attempted(is_retry=true)` and a terminal result carrying the same retry classification. Ordinary first sign-in remains `false`; validation and cancellation do not count as attempts.
- **Actual (pre-fix):** Samsung confirmed the bug: the Retry route was identified by the `retry_started` breadcrumb, but the accepted submit was `is_retry=false`; authentication failed and the failed-card state remained recoverable. Zero PID-scoped exception-report markers and fatal crashes were observed.
- **Frequency:** Every card-level retry that creates a new Login destination after a failed attempt; direct retries within the same Login ViewModel already use its local marker.
- **Evidence:** [Samsung pre-fix Retry event capture](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-449-retry-attribution-prefx.txt); [sanitized source-audit trace](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-449-retry-attribution-source-audit.txt); `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementScreen.kt` Retry action; `feature/settings/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/settings/ui/servers/ServerManagementViewModel.kt` login navigation; `composeApp/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/parrot/navigation/RootNavigationViewModel.kt` destination creation; `feature/login/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/login/ui/login/LoginViewModel.kt` `lastFailedLogin` and `beginLoginAttempt`.
- **Root cause:** Retry semantics are held only in the Login ViewModel, while retry navigation leaves and recreates that ViewModel.
- **Affected files:** Login destination/navigation retry metadata and Login ViewModel attempt dimension; corresponding tests and case-449 evidence.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `2d4360996a620f4cba2d25c25bbd3e7cdaaeba88` (`fix(login): preserve retry attribution across reauth [QA-BUG-0052, case 449]`). The failed-card Retry origin is carried as a bounded Boolean through the Login route and consumed only when the first valid, gated authentication submit starts. Route-level `server_login_attempted` and login started/succeeded/failed/cancelled/abandoned events now retain the bounded `is_retry` Boolean; no server identifier is added. Regression tests cover first-submit consumption, normal first attempt and same-route retry.
- **Retest:** PASS on Samsung candidate matching committed QA-BUG-0052 source: `:feature:login:ui:testAndroidHostTest`, `:feature:settings:ui:testAndroidHostTest`, `:lib:analytics:implementation:testAndroidHostTest`, `:composeApp:testAndroidHostTest`, and `:androidApp:assembleDebug --rerun-tasks` all succeeded (602 tasks). Installed package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), PID `1802`, local/installed APK SHA-256 `f3c4eb2c62bc21aec91c3c32f583227760d033b4591bd0ace804b15b8de58a65`; built before commit from identical QA-BUG-0052 source plus the separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 candidate. Samsung event sequence: normal card Log in → `server_login_attempted(is_retry=false)` → `login_attempted(false)` → `login_failed(false)`; after returning to the failed card, Retry → `server_login_attempted(true)`; an empty submit emitted `login_validation_failed` only; the next accepted submit emitted `login_attempted(true)` → `login_failed(true)`. Retry then Back without submitting emitted only the route attempt and returned to the retained failure card. Local diagnostics showed the bounded retry/authentication/failure-state breadcrumbs and zero exception-report markers/fatal crashes. Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived; Crashlytics delivery is not established by debug logs. The configured endpoint rejected the synthetic test values, so successful authentication remains untested; no input values were retained. [Samsung retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-449-retry-attribution-retest.txt).

## QA-BUG-0053 — Profile management operations lack correct attempt/outcome and failure instrumentation

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium for observability; profile switching/creation/rename/delete can fail or partially complete without an outcome, recovery breadcrumb, user-visible error, or useful diagnostic report. The existing switch event is emitted before persistence and can count a no-op/failure as success.
- **Status:** FIXED IN SOURCE (`b428b334`); PARTIAL SAMSUNG RETEST. Visible-route exposure, profile menu/dialog presentation and ordinary dismissal variants passed. Profile mutation success/failure/retry variants remain NOT RUN, so the screen is not signed off.
- **Screen/test IDs:** App Settings/Profile cases 408–422; operation instrumentation cases 534, 740–750 where applicable.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0 (`S921BXXSGDZG1`); package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), PID `1802`, APK SHA-256 `f3c4eb2c62bc21aec91c3c32f583227760d033b4591bd0ace804b15b8de58a65`; source `2d4360996a620f4cba2d25c25bbd3e7cdaaeba88` plus separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 candidate.
- **Preconditions:** App Settings/Profile section visible; source flow uses `UserRegistry` preference persistence for profile mutations.
- **Exact reproduction / audit:** Samsung Settings → App Settings exposed profile tiles and Add profile. PID-scoped local records showed `tab_switch_attempted`/`tab_switched` and App Settings observation/route breadcrumbs, but no App Settings/Profile visible-destination event. Source audit: `HomeNavigation` reports actual-visible Server Management only; `AppSettingsViewModel.selectProfile` emits `profile_switched(profile_id)` before launching `setActiveProfile`; create/rename/delete have no attempted or cancellation outcome, and their `viewModelScope.launch` bodies do not catch registry exceptions. Profile event payloads use private IDs/names, which are stripped by the analytics provider allowlist, leaving the events without useful bounded dimensions.
- **Expected:** One visible App Settings exposure event per actual route exposure; committed profile operations report bounded attempt and terminal success/failure/cancellation with a per-operation diagnostic correlation ID. User-authored names and profile identifiers must not enter Analytics or diagnostic messages. Unexpected persistence failures should leave the existing active profile/state usable, show a recoverable error, and be reported once at the UI operation boundary; ordinary Back/Cancel is not a Crashlytics issue.
- **Actual:** App Settings/Profile exposure lacks its own screen-view event; switch is reported before the asynchronous write is known to succeed; create/rename/delete emit only a post-operation legacy event and do not handle failure/cancellation or show recoverable error state. No device-side persistence failure has yet been induced.
- **Frequency:** Every current App Settings exposure lacks a screen-view event; every selected profile switch is reported before persistence; every failed or cancelled create/rename/delete can escape without a terminal signal.
- **Evidence:** [Case-408 profile exposure/source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-408-profile-exposure-prefx.txt); `HomeNavigation.kt` visible-destination effect; `AppSettingsViewModel.kt` profile handlers; `UserRegistry.kt` and `UserRegistryImpl.kt` persistence contract.
- **Root cause:** Profile actions are implemented as direct `viewModelScope.launch` calls without the operation wrapper/recovery pattern used by current-book clearing; the Home visible-route observer handles Server Management but not App Settings.
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/navigation/HomeNavigation.kt`, `HomeNavigationViewModel.kt`, `appsettings/AppSettingsViewModel.kt`/view state/screen; typed App Settings Analytics events and provider tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `b428b334` (`fix(settings): instrument profile operations [QA-BUG-0053]`). Adds actual-visible route exposure; bounded typed operation/dialog/menu events without profile names or IDs; attempt/success/failure/cancellation outcomes; correlated unexpected-failure breadcrumbs and one operation-boundary exception report; retryable visible failure state; and local regression tests. Ordinary user cancellation rethrows cancellation without a non-fatal.
- **Retest:** Partial PASS on Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`: local-visible Settings entry emitted exactly one `app_settings_screen_viewed` and one route breadcrumb; Add and Rename opened/cancelled via button, system Back and outside tap; blank Add remained unsubmitted; Edit and long-press menus emitted distinct sources; menu outside/System-Back dismissal emitted bounded `dismiss_request`; zero profile mutation/attempt events, exception markers or fatal crashes. Host operation tests covered one attempt/terminal success, one unexpected failure/report, cancellation without non-fatal, retry attribution, and profile data exclusion. Samsung mutation-success/failure paths remain NOT RUN until the QA-BUG-0056 guard is committed and a disposable profile fixture is safely available. Exact device/build evidence: [case 408/759 retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-408-759-profile-exposure-dismissal-retest.txt). Debug events are local-only; Firebase Analytics ingestion is waived and Crashlytics Console delivery remains unverified.

## QA-BUG-0054 — Profile names and identifiers are written to application logs

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium privacy risk; user-authored profile names and internal profile identifiers can appear in local Kermit/device logs or shared diagnostic files.
- **Status:** FIXED IN SOURCE (`9e0f707b`); Samsung startup-load and runtime create/activate/switch/rename/delete registry-log redaction checks PASS. Preference-cleanup and shared-file/Firebase diagnostic delivery are not covered by this runtime check.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Profile cases 408–416 and 422; sensitive-data case 509.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0; startup-only retest PID `23557`, APK SHA-256 `bebdd5542c81be20b8b6d58d8eb904d6f2e68e40ceda7b6bcc998c575376d9d5`; runtime mutation retest PID `14481`, Parrot 0.4.5 (21), APK SHA-256 `319fba7efbd020c8b799ecb82f5af249e99b89119b3a4749ffe939ec626b1a7c`, source includes fix `9e0f707b` and later profile instrumentation plus separate QA-BUG-0049 work. Local and Samsung hashes match.
- **Preconditions:** Profile registry loads, creates, updates, deletes, or activates a profile while debug/file logging is enabled.
- **Exact reproduction / audit:** Source inspection found `UserRegistryImpl` debug logs interpolate the display name for create/update/activate/delete and the active profile ID on load; delete also logs the profile ID. No mutation was performed to produce a raw runtime line, and no profile name or ID has been copied into this register.
- **Expected:** Registry logs contain only stable operation names and non-sensitive aggregate state; no profile names, UUIDs, IDs, credentials, or other user-authored values.
- **Actual:** Kermit log message templates contain profile names and/or IDs. Whether an individual value reached a shared diagnostic file during this run is not established.
- **Frequency:** Every applicable create/update/delete/activate operation and startup load with an active profile.
- **Evidence:** `lib/user/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/user/implementation/UserRegistryImpl.kt` lines 39–50, 63–108, and 132–150; [case-408 profile exposure/source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-408-profile-exposure-prefx.txt).
- **Root cause:** Debug logging interpolates profile domain fields rather than stable operation-only messages.
- **Affected files:** `lib/user/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/user/implementation/UserRegistryImpl.kt` and targeted source/log-sanitization checks.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `9e0f707b` (`fix(privacy): redact profile registry log values [QA-BUG-0054]`). Registry create/update/delete/activate/load/missing-profile/cleanup messages are now stable and contain no profile name or identifier; aggregate profile counts remain available.
- **Retest (startup path, historical):** PASS on Samsung PID `23557`, hash `bebdd554…d9d5`; two safe UserRegistry records, zero UUID-pattern matches, zero legacy value-template matches, zero exception/fatal markers. See [startup log retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-509-profile-logs-redacted-retest.txt). This earlier run did not exercise mutations.
- **Retest (runtime mutations):** PASS for create, activation/switch, rename and deletion on Samsung PID `14481`, package 0.4.5 (21), APK SHA-256 `319fba7efbd020c8b799ecb82f5af249e99b89119b3a4749ffe939ec626b1a7c`. A scan of 17 PID-scoped UserRegistry records found zero matches for either synthetic profile label and zero UUID-like registry values; correlated operation IDs in diagnostic breadcrumbs are contract-approved diagnostic context and are not profile IDs or Analytics dimensions. Zero exception/fatal markers. Raw log lines and values were not retained. Evidence: [profile mutation and redaction retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/profile-switch-rename-delete-retest.txt). Preference-cleanup runtime paths and Firebase/Crashlytics delivery remain unverified.

## QA-BUG-0055 — Deleting the active profile may leave the app without an active profile

- **Severity / user impact:** High; deleting the active profile leaves no active database context, and the automatic Home/Books reset crashes Parrot. The user cannot continue in a remaining profile until recovery.
- **Status:** FIXED in commit `64806d33cdb67c633d44d25d530bd0539d97f4b6`; Samsung post-fix retest PASS (1/1). The pre-fix Samsung crash remains preserved below.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Profiles cases 412 and 422; profile persistence/recovery cases 409 and 419–421.
- **Device/build/commit (pre-fix):** Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0; package `com.retro99.parrot` 0.4.5 (21), PID `15709`, APK SHA-256 `3d01dec69e98f5f2bcfef76faced86aee9284015e552b3499f6b324c0bbc675b`, based on QA-BUG-0066 commit `4f49c80c` plus unrelated uncommitted Home/Reader/Server changes. No app data was cleared; the preserved profile was not selected or deleted.
- **Device/build/commit (post-fix):** Same Samsung/package/version; PID `11251`, APK SHA-256 `75e418871ec005041a2f01259c080662bf4009d76aca7bef3763fd34f8916fd3`. APK was built from commit `9789f5a1` plus then-uncommitted QA-BUG-0055 changes and unrelated Home/Reader/Server work; those QA-BUG-0055 changes were committed after successful retest as `64806d33cdb67c633d44d25d530bd0539d97f4b6`. Installed with `adb install -r`, without clearing data.
- **Preconditions:** At least two profiles exist; the profile selected for deletion is active. Use only a disposable profile with no servers/books.
- **Exact reproduction:** On the Samsung in App Settings, while a disposable profile was active and other profiles (including the preserved original) remained, opened that profile's Edit menu → Delete → confirmed Delete. App emitted `profile_operation_attempted` and `profile_deleted` with a matching bounded `profile_delete` correlation; immediately afterward Android displayed “Parrot closed because this app has a bug.” No profile labels or identifiers are retained.
- **Expected:** Deletion either selects a remaining profile before removing the active one or follows another safe documented policy; Parrot remains usable, the active database context is valid, and no unrelated/default profile data is exposed.
- **Actual (pre-fix):** The delete operation cleared the active ID and emitted a success terminal. `HomeNavigation` reset to Books; constructing `BooksListViewModel` then threw `IllegalStateException: No active user profile. Cannot access database.` from `DatabaseManager.getDatabase`, causing a fatal main-thread process crash. No app data was cleared; the preserved profile was not selected or deleted. Frequency: 1/1 pre-fix attempt.
- **Actual (post-fix):** A newly created, empty disposable active profile was deleted while three other profiles remained. The app returned to a usable Books list with a valid remaining profile active; App Settings confirmed exactly one fewer profile, with the preserved baseline profile still present and active. No crash or exception marker occurred. Frequency: 1/1 post-fix retest.
- **Evidence:** [Case-422 pre-fix crash](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-28/qa-bug-0055-case-422-active-profile-delete-prefx.txt) and [post-fix Samsung/host retest](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-28/qa-bug-0055-case-422-active-profile-delete-retest.txt). Source audit: `AppSettingsViewModel.deleteProfile`; `UserRegistryImpl.deleteProfile`; `HomeNavigationViewModel` profile observer; `BooksListViewModel.observeFavorites`.
- **Root cause:** Pre-fix deletion cleared the active ID before Home reset and did not choose a remaining profile; the next Books ViewModel requires an active database context. The fix, under the registry mutex, chooses the most recently used remaining profile (creation time breaks ties), updates its last-active time, and activates it before deleting the old profile. If no fallback exists, registry deletion is rejected rather than clearing the only active context. App Settings verifies deletion/fallback postconditions before reporting success.
- **Diagnostics / analytics observed:** Post-fix Samsung emitted exactly one delete attempt and one `profile_deleted` success; one started and one terminal breadcrumb shared a correlation ID, and the terminal carried bounded `reason_code=active_profile_fallback_activated`. Registry logged one operation-only fallback message. No profile name/ID appeared in retained evidence. No fatal/AndroidRuntime exception marker. Firebase Analytics ingestion remains waived; Crashlytics backend delivery is unverified (no failure was induced).
- **Affected files:** `feature/home/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/home/ui/appsettings/AppSettingsViewModel.kt`, `ProfileOperation.kt`, their focused tests, and `lib/user/implementation/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/retro99/user/implementation/UserRegistryImpl.kt` plus registry regressions.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `64806d33cdb67c633d44d25d530bd0539d97f4b6` — `fix(profiles): activate fallback before deleting current profile [QA-BUG-0055, test 422]`.
- **Retest:** PASS on the physical Samsung (case 422, 1/1 post-fix). Registry regressions pass for active fallback, inactive deletion, last-profile protection and observer state; the profile-operation test verifies the bounded fallback reason is attached to the correlated terminal breadcrumb. Full Home UI host suite: 28 tests; UserRegistry host suite: 4 tests; zero failures/errors/skips. Debug APK assembly passed. No data clear/reinstall; only a newly created empty disposable profile was removed. [Retest evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-28/qa-bug-0055-case-422-active-profile-delete-retest.txt).

## QA-BUG-0056 — Profile mutations can be submitted repeatedly while persistence is in flight

- **Severity / user impact:** Medium, confirmed by source audit; rapid confirmation taps can launch concurrent create/rename/delete calls, duplicate profiles, race profile state, or emit multiple outcomes. Rapid profile selection can also race active-profile writes/navigation resets.
- **Status:** FIXED IN SOURCE (`7669f849`) with partial Samsung retest. One rapid Add burst created exactly one profile and one intended auto-switch; UI pending state was too brief to observe. QA-BUG-0058 tail-tap defect was separately fixed/retested in `63306140`. Catalogue case 743 remains incomplete because slow-pending, Rename/Delete, open/retry and switch-race variants have not been exercised.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Profiles cases 409, 411–413; shared repeated-action case 743.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0; installed Parrot 0.4.5 (21), PID `7916`, APK SHA-256 `4b72393d8e0eabc532317c0f9ecc28f4a09e19ba868ca1d644ed43d53d641820`; source `63306140` (QA-BUG-0058), `7669f849` (QA-BUG-0056), `c0d8545d` (QA-BUG-0057), plus separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 work. A synthetic disposable profile was created/removed; original profile remains active.
- **Preconditions:** Profile screen is visible; a create/rename/delete write or profile switch has not yet returned.
- **Exact reproduction / audit:** Pre-fix source audit found no in-flight gate, duplicate-submit guard or disabled controls. On the guarded Samsung candidate, opened Add Profile, entered a synthetic disposable name, hid the keyboard and sent two taps at the confirm button with a 1 ms interval. Logs showed exactly one Create attempt/success and one automatic Switch attempt/success; no duplicate profile was created. The whole create/switch completed within about 8 ms, so a slow pending operation/UI-disabled interval was not observed. The tail tap reached an underlying setting (separate QA-BUG-0058) and was restored immediately.
- **Expected:** At most one accepted operation is in flight; repeated taps do not cause duplicate writes, stale profile callbacks, multiple routes or false success events. Cancel/dismiss cannot be mislabeled while a committed mutation is still running.
- **Actual:** Pre-fix handlers start a new coroutine for each intent and leave their controls enabled. Concurrent registry writes are therefore possible.
- **Frequency:** Every operation while its persistence call remains pending; rapid-switch race depends on scheduling.
- **Evidence:** [Case-743 profile repeated-submit source audit](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/case-743-profile-repeated-submit-prefx.txt); `AppSettingsViewModel.kt` profile handlers and `AppSettingsScreen.kt` dialog/tile controls.
- **Root cause:** No ViewModel/UI in-flight guard around suspend profile mutations.
- **Affected files:** App Settings profile ViewModel/state/screen and host regression tests.
- **Fix reference / commit:** `7669f849` (`fix(settings): guard profile operations in flight [QA-BUG-0056]`). Adds a single in-flight guard around profile writes/switches, rejects duplicate intents, disables profile controls and dialog dismissal/inputs while pending, and shows operation progress. Ordinary cancellation remains available before an operation is accepted.
- **Retest:** `:feature:home:ui:testAndroidHostTest`, analytics host tests and Android debug assembly PASS. Samsung candidate produced one Create and one automatic Switch for a two-tap Add burst; temporary profile was switched away from and deleted, leaving original profile active. `63306140` subsequently shielded the tail tap; see [QA-BUG-0058 post-fix evidence](manual-qa-evidence/2026-09-27/qa-bug-0058-profile-submit-tail-shield-retest.txt). Pending-state visuals, slow storage, repeated Rename/Delete and switch-race/open/retry variants remain NOT RUN because no delayed-operation fixture is available. Case 743 remains NOT RUN for those uncovered variants; the repeated-Add subvariant is PASS.

## QA-BUG-0057 — Profile retry attribution leaks across unrelated operations

- **Severity / user impact:** Low for direct functionality, medium for telemetry correctness; distinct profile operations can be counted as retries even when the earlier failure belonged to another profile or another dialog session, distorting retry and completion rates.
- **Status:** FIXED IN SOURCE (`c0d8545d`); host regression tests PASS; Samsung installed-build smoke PASS. A true failed-write retry event sequence remains BLOCKED because no disposable profile/fault fixture is available; no mutation or failure was induced.
- **Screen/test IDs:** Profiles cases 409, 411, 413; shared operation outcome/retry cases 743 and 749.
- **Device/build/commit:** Samsung serial `RFCWC0SSVDM`, SM-S921B, Android 16/API 36, One UI 8.0; installed Parrot 0.4.5 (21), PID `10798`, APK SHA-256 `9583b61421c46d930c1cf157529a3d0546d6a6a7b17c35c3f85aced299e6c6f3`, source commit `b428b334` plus separate uncommitted QA-BUG-0049 candidate. No profile mutation or failure was attempted.
- **Preconditions:** A profile operation fails; before a matching logical operation is retried, the user opens a new dialog/chooses another profile and starts the same operation type.
- **Exact reproduction / audit:** Source `AppSettingsViewModel.runProfileOperation` tracks failures in `failedProfileOperations`, a set keyed only by `ProfileOperation` enum.
